## 239. Visual Infographic of Ancient Chinese Official Ranks and Attire

- **id**：4966
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=4966

### 描述

This is a detailed, historical infographic prompt designed to visualize and break down the ranking system of official attire in ancient China, specifically focusing on the Qing Dynasty. It requests a 16:9 horizontal composition in the style of ancient historical diagrams and illustrated books, comparing the uniforms of nine officials from the first to the ninth rank. The prompt specifies detailed elements like the embroidered rank badges (Buzi), court beads (Chaozhu), hat ornaments (Dingdai Hualing), and dragon robes (Mangpao), along with explanatory tables and historical context, making it suitable for educational content on Chinese history and culture.

### 示例图

![Visual Infographic of Ancient Chinese Official Ranks and Attire](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1767804124358_rj8md2_G9-kOhCbQAAeqc-.jpg)

### 提示词

```
Create a historical diagram-style infographic, 16:9 horizontal composition, titled "Visual Deconstruction of the Ancient Chinese Official Attire Ranking System." Adopt the aesthetic of ancient canonical diagrams and draw in the style of illustrations from ancient books. The image should display a comparison of the official uniforms of nine ranks of officials from the {argument name="dynasty" default="Qing Dynasty"}, arranged from left to right by rank: nine standing figures from the first to the ninth rank. Above each figure, use fine-line painting (Gongbi) to label the "Buzi (Rank Badge) Pattern" (First Rank Crane, Second Rank Golden Pheasant, Third Rank Peacock... Ninth Rank Quail; civil officials use flying birds, military officials use fierce beasts), with a close-up magnifying glass effect highlighting the embroidery details of the Buzi. Below the figures, label the "Chaozhu (Court Bead) Material" (First Rank Eastern Pearl, Second Rank Coral, Third Rank Sapphire... decreasing by rank), "Dingdai Hualing (Hat Ornament)" (Ruby/Coral/Sapphire/Crystal, etc., distinguished by color blocks), and "Mangpao (Dragon Robe) Pattern" (Nine Dragons/Five Claws/Four Claws, using diagrams to illustrate the difference in claw count). The top of the image features a "Rank Identification Quick Reference Chart": a table comparing the differences across 5 dimensions (Buzi, Dingdai, Chaozhu, Mangpao, Waist Belt) for all 9 ranks, with each cell marked by a small icon and text. The bottom of the image features "Historical Trivia": labeling "Why civil officials used flying birds and military officials used fierce beasts" (civil service requires soaring high, military service requires bravery), "The political meaning of the Hualing" (being granted a Hualing signifies imperial favor), and "Color Restrictions" (commoners were forbidden from wearing yellow or bright yellow). The very bottom includes a timeline showing the "Evolution of Official Attire through Dynasties": thumbnail sketches illustrating the style changes from the Han, Tang, Song, Yuan, Ming, and Qing dynasties. All text must be in Chinese: the top title uses the Small Seal Script font "Official Attire Rank Diagram: The Power Code Worn on the Body," and the bottom caption states "The clothing system is the visual representation of the feudal hierarchy." Use traditional official attire colors for the palette. The overall presentation should focus on historical institutions and clothing culture, suitable for historical education and cultural research.
```

## 242. Scientific Theory Visualization Generator

- **id**：4723
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=4723

### 描述

A complex, multi-phase prompt designed to visualize abstract scientific theories or formulas (like black holes) as photorealistic, impossible architectural structures. It starts with a 2D notation medium (e.g., chalk on a blackboard) and extrudes the lines into a solid 3D structure, featuring a tiny scientist observing the creation.

### 示例图

![Scientific Theory Visualization Generator](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1767682094478_zeo8ac_G9xfX3gWwAAZm_W.jpg)

### 提示词

```
<instructions> do this for {argument name="scientific concept" default="black holes"}
  Input Variable:   {argument name="theorem or formula" default="[Theorem / Formula]"}
Phase 1: Schematic Decomposition
Analyze the Notation: Identify the primary visual language of the scientist (Chalk on blackboard? Ink on parchment? Pencil on graph paper?).
Identify the "Structure": If this theory were a building, what would it look like?
Logic: Chaos Theory -> Fractal Spire. Thermodynamics -> Engine Block.
Phase 2: The Dimensional Transition
The Base: A flat surface covered in the [Notation Medium] (e.g., a slate blackboard covered in chalk equations).
The Extrusion: The chalk lines physically lift off the surface. They begin as white wireframes, then gain opacity, then gain texture.
The Climax: At the top of the image, the wireframes have fully solidified into a photorealistic, impossible structure representing the theory.
The Observer: A tiny, scale-model figure of the scientist stands on the flat "2D" section, looking up in awe at the massive "3D" structure they have summoned.
Output: High-Angle Isometric, "Architectural Visualization" style, stark contrast between sketchy lines and solid mass.
</instructions>
```

## 244. Seed to Shelf Diorama Flowchart Prompt

- **id**：4808
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=4808

### 描述

A complex, multi-step instruction prompt for visualizing the lifecycle of a product (like coffee or a diamond ring) using a miniature photography aesthetic. It requires generating a single image featuring a 'Seed to Shelf' flowchart diorama on a vintage map, with three distinct 3D vignettes (Origin, Process, Destination) connected by a winding conveyor belt filled with the product, all rendered at HO Scale (1:87) with tilt-shift effect.

### 示例图

![Seed to Shelf Diorama Flowchart Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1767682202355_t27yr6_G9y3TexW8AAKh1K.jpg)

### 提示词

```
<instruction>
  1. Analyze Input Variable:  
Input A is a Global Commodity with a distinct production lifecycle (e.g., Coffee, Chocolate/Cocoa, Wine, Denim/Cotton).
Deconstruct the lifecycle into   3 Distinct Stages  :
   Stage 1 (The Origin):  The agricultural source (e.g., Coffee Plantation, Cacao Trees).
   Stage 2 (The Process):  The industrial transformation (e.g., Roasting Plant, Fermentation Vats).
   Stage 3 (The Destination):  The retail experience (e.g., A Hipster Cafe, A Chocolatier Shop).
2.  Container (The Map Table):
Goal: A "Seed to Shelf" Flowchart Diorama.
The Base: A large, vintage World Map (blue oceans, pastel continents) laid flat on a wooden dining table.
The Spine: A raised, winding S-Shaped Chute/Conveyor snakes through the center of the image, physically connecting the three stages.
The Flow: The chute is filled with the product, which visually transforms as it moves down the path (e.g., Red Coffee Cherries at the top 
→
→
 Brown Roasted Beans in the middle 
→
→
 Fine Ground Powder at the bottom).
3. The Vignettes (The Stations):
Construct a 3D diorama "Island" at each key point of the S-curve:
Top Station (Origin): A square patch of terrain sitting on the map (e.g., South America). Detailed crops, trees, and farmers harvesting the raw material.
Middle Station (Process): A compact industrial facility sitting on the map (e.g., Europe/USA). Metallic roasters, silos, smoke stacks, and factory workers.
Bottom Station (Destination): An architectural Cutaway Box. The front wall is removed to reveal the interior of a shop (e.g., Espresso machines, tiny customers sitting at tables, baristas serving).
4. The Narrative Scale (The Workers):
Scale: 1:87 (HO Scale) miniature figures.
Action: The figures are not just standing; they are actively moving the product from the "Station" into the "Chute" (e.g., Farmers dumping baskets of cherries into the stream, Factory workers shoveling beans).
5. Lighting & Atmosphere:
Camera Angle: High-Angle Perspective (45-60 degrees). Looking down the length of the table.
Lighting: Natural Daylight. Bright, cheerful, and even. Shadows fall softly on the map.
Depth of Field: Tilt-Shift effect. The top and bottom edges of the table are slightly blurred to make the models look tiny.
Output: ONE image, Vertical Aspect Ratio (4:5), Miniature Photography, Educational Diorama aesthetic.
</instruction>
```

## 248. Kinetic Sculpture of Scientific Discovery in a Lab Notebook

- **id**：4604
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=4604

### 描述

A highly creative and detailed prompt for generating a single image that visualizes a scientific discovery as a kinetic sculpture rising from an aged lab notebook. It requires blending steampunk-inspired brass and glass apparatus with handwritten notes and sketches, including a tiny figurine of the scientist and a subtle glowing element to symbolize the 'eureka' moment, rendered in an industrial scientific photography style.

### 示例图

![Kinetic Sculpture of Scientific Discovery in a Lab Notebook](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1767606952844_sl59q1_G9yzBN_asAEpf5r.jpg)

### 提示词

```
Role:
Science Historian & Kinetic Sculpture Engineer

Input:
Scientific Discovery: {argument name="scientific discovery" default="{{DISCOVERY}}"}
Primary Scientist: {argument name="scientist name" default="{{SCIENTIST_NAME}}"}

Year: {{YEAR}}
Lab Location: {{LOCATION}}

Phase 1: Concept Analysis

Identify the defining eureka moment behind {{DISCOVERY}}
Briefly imply earlier failed attempts through visual cues

Select the key physical apparatus used or symbolized in this discovery (e.g. telescope, prism, microscope, equation, clock, lens)

Phase 2: Visual Execution

Goal:
A single 1x1 frame showing a working “lab notebook contraption” frozen mid-discovery.

Scene Rules:

Base:
An open, aged lab notebook on a wooden desk. Coffee stains, handwritten equations, rough sketches, and marginal notes fill the pages.

The Machine:
A functional brass and glass apparatus rises directly from the notebook pages. Steampunk-inspired but scientifically grounded.

The Process:
The apparatus actively demonstrates {{DISCOVERY}}

Examples: light bending, gears rotating, particles flowing, rings orbiting, waves oscillating.

The Scientist:
A tiny, realistic figurine of {{SCIENTIST_NAME}} in period-accurate lab clothing, holding a fountain pen and taking notes.

The Failure:
Crossed-out equations, torn sketches, and crumpled paper scattered near the notebook.

The Eureka:
One subtle glowing element (LED, filament, beam of light, energized ring) symbolizing the breakthrough moment.

The Label:
Handwritten in fountain pen style on the notebook page:
“{{SCIENTIST_NAME}} | {{DISCOVERY}} | {{YEAR}} | {{LOCATION}}”

Era Props:
Period-accurate tools such as a slide rule, telegram, brass compass, glass tubes, Bunsen burner, vacuum tubes, or notebooks.

Style & Output

Composition: Single frame, centered subject, square 1x1

Lighting: Dramatic side lighting, warm highlights, deep shadows

Texture: Brass, glass, paper fibers, ink stains clearly visible

Photography Style: Industrial scientific photography, cinematic realism

Mood: Quiet intensity, intellectual tension, frozen moment of discovery
Quality: Ultra-detailed, macro realism, shallow depth of field

-   The Props:   Era-accurate items (telegram, slide rule, Bunsen burner, petri dishes)

  Output:   2x2 Grid, Industrial Scientific Photography, dramatic side lighting
</instructions>
```

## 251. Procedural Book Nook Diorama Generation Prompt

- **id**：4504
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=4504

### 描述

A highly structured, procedural prompt template designed to generate a crowded, hyper-realistic 'Book Nook' diorama based on a user-defined topic. It uses semantic analysis to define 'The Totem' and 'The Ephemera' assets, enforces a strict 90-degree corner geometry, and specifies lighting and texture contrast to create an optical illusion.

### 示例图

![Procedural Book Nook Diorama Generation Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1767604000544_yhrl9i_G9mPO2WXgAAXUg8.jpg)

![Procedural Book Nook Diorama Generation Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1767604002263_081eax_G9mP6egXgAIP9aZ.jpg)

### 提示词

```
<instructions>    Input Variable:   [INSERT TOPIC HERE] (e.g., History of Space Exploration)

  System Instruction:  
Generate a crowded, hyper-realistic   "Book Nook" Diorama  . Use the following logic to procedurally fill the scene with high-density detail:

  1. Semantic Analysis (The Loot Table):  
      The Totem:   Identify the single tallest, most vertical object representing the topic (e.g., Saturn V Rocket).
      The Ephemera:   Identify 4-5 small, specific consumer goods or artifacts (e.g., "Tang" Packet, Freeze-Dried Ice Cream, Mission Patches, Apollo 11 Manual).
      The Timeline:   Identify the key visual evolution steps (e.g., Sputnik -> Lunar Module -> Shuttle).

  2. The Immutable Container (The Corner Stage):  
      Geometry:   The book is NOT lying flat. It is standing upright, opened to form a   Strict 90-Degree Corner  .
         Vertical Wall:  The back cover/pages standing straight up.
         Horizontal Floor:  The front cover/pages lying flat.
      The Anchor:   The "Totem" object (Rocket) stands   inside the spine crease  , acting as the structural pillar holding the book open.
      Paper Texture:   The pages must look thick, rough, and heavily aged (like an 1800s manuscript).

  3. The Vertical Plane (The Da Vinci Sketchbook):  
      Density:     MAXIMUM.   Do not leave white space.
      Style:   Sepia-tone   Pen & Ink Technical Drawings  .
      Content:   The background should be a chaotic but organized collage of diagrams, timelines, and schematics. It should look like a scientist's field notes.
      Text:   A bold, formal Serif Title at the top (e.g., "{argument name="title" default="HISTORY OF SPACE EXPLORATION"}").

  4. The Horizontal Plane (The Physical Clutter):  
      Transition:   The Totem (Rocket) seamlessly transitions from a drawing on the wall to a   Physical 3D Model   on the floor.
      The Scatter:   The "Ephemera" props are   scattered and overlapping   on the floor page. They should not be neatly arranged.
         Material Contrast:  The props must look like shiny plastic, crinkled foil, and fabric patches. This clashes with the matte paper.
         Scale:  A tiny 1:64 scale human figure (e.g., Astronaut) stands next to the massive Totem for scale.

  5. Visual Syntax:  
      Background:     Void Black.   The book floats in infinite darkness.   DO NOT render studio equipment, lamps, or tables.  
      Lighting:   "Museum Display" lighting. A focused beam hits the Totem, creating a long shadow against the back page sketches.
      Depth:   The 3D objects must cast hard shadows onto the 2D paper to sell the optical illusion.

  Output:   1:1 Aspect Ratio, Macro Photography, High Textural Contrast (Paper vs. Plastic). </instructions>
```

## 257. Vertical Layered Diorama Concept Art Prompt for Sci-Fi Film

- **id**：4448
- **需要参考图**：是
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=4448

### 描述

An extremely detailed meta-prompt for Nano Banana Pro (using Thinking Mode) combining 'Gravity Prompt' and 'Movie Vertical Miniature Prompt' to generate a complex, vertical, layered diorama visualization of a fictional sci-fi film titled 'GRAVITY BROKEN TOKYO'. The prompt specifies roles, steps, visual styles, gravity effects, and typography.

### 示例图

![Vertical Layered Diorama Concept Art Prompt for Sci-Fi Film](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1767508503563_7t5rzj_G9q1el9aUAAcYut.jpg)

### 提示词

```
You are an **architect and concept artist who visualizes story structures**.
Analyze the provided input text (the movie synopsis) and generate a **"Vertical Layered Diorama"** where the height direction represents the timeline.

The character in the attached reference image should be placed in the "Present (Middle Layer)" or "Future (Top Layer)" of this vertical structure, acting as the **storyteller/time traveler**, and posed to **look down on the past / look up at the future**.

---

## ▼ Instructions for Generation Process (Nano Banana Pro: Thinking Mode)

1. **[Step 1: Timeline Mapping]**: Decompose the synopsis into three time phases and assign them to physical layers.

   * **Bottom Layer (Past/Origin)**: Cause of gravity collapse, sealing, deep underground. Dark and heavy tone.
   * **Middle Layer (Present/Conflict)**: Survival and conflict in gravity-broken Tokyo. Vivid and dynamic tone.
   * **Top Layer (Future/Conclusion)**: Birth of a new gravity order, hope or ruin. Open and symbolic tone.
2. **[Step 2: Connection Design]**: Design the layers so they are not disconnected, but physically connected by elements such as **Gravity Well pillars / inverted spiral staircases / magnetic elevators / waterfalls where falling turns into rising**.
3. **[Step 3: Text Integration]**: Place the movie title in a stable location at the very top or bottom, and engrave famous lines on the **layer boundaries**.

---

## ▼ Design and Style Specification

* **Composition/Layout**: **Vertical Cross-section / Tower structure**. Aspect ratio is vertical (**9:16**). Viewpoint is **slightly above the front**, at an angle that shows the thickness and depth of the layers.
* **Taste**: **Precise museum model, geological cross-section, surreal architecture**. Express different eras and materials in each layer (concrete → neon metal → white structure on clouds) while integrating them as one giant object.
* **Texture/Image Quality**: Photorealistic, precise detail, **8k**. Raindrops, wet asphalt, reflections, fog, volumetric light.
* **Gravity Expression (Important)**:

  * Tokyo roads **curve 90 degrees as a continuous surface**, where the ground becomes a wall, and then arches to the ceiling, connecting in a **loop**.
  * Buildings, signs, cars, and pipes **protrude vertically** from the curved surface, and landmarks exist **upside down** suspended in the air.
  * Depict as a **physically continuous urban structure**, not just a reflection.
  * Light sources match the reality of a rainy night (reflections of neon and streetlights, diffused light through clouds).

---

## ▼ Input Text (Fictional Movie)

**Title**: **{argument name="Movie Title" default="GRAVITY BROKEN TOKYO (Gravity Fault City Tokyo)"}**
**Genre**: Sci-Fi Thriller / Disaster / Human Drama

**Synopsis**:
One night, due to the fracture of the "Gravity Lattice" above Tokyo, the city's gravity lost its uniformity. Roads bend into the sky, buildings lose their up and down, and falling chooses a direction. People survive by redefining "where the ground is." The narrator (reference character) dives across time to the origin of the gravity collapse, struggles in the current city, and reaches the new order of the future.

### [Bottom Layer / Past and Origin] (PHASE 1: YEAR 2036)

* **Setting**: Deep underground research complex. Black basalt-like strata, old wiring, gravity control reactor "Atlas Core."
* **Description**: Suggests that the device meant to "fix" gravity instead triggered the "tearing" of gravity. Tools and paper documents are scattered on the floor, and water droplets float horizontally due to gravity disturbances.
* **Tone**: Dark, oppressive. Warning lights, fine dust, the stillness of low gravity.

### [Middle Layer / Present and Conflict] (PHASE 2: YEAR 2046)

* **Setting**: The center of gravity-broken Tokyo, where rain continues to fall. A continuous city where **wet asphalt curves 90 degrees, becoming a wall, and continuing to the ceiling**.
* **Description (Reflecting Gravity Prompt)**:

  * Roads rise vertically and then arch overhead to loop.
  * Landmarks are suspended upside down, and road signs protrude into the air.
  * Neon lights bleed along the curved surfaces in the fog and light rain, and reflections establish the light of a real rainy night.
* **Character Placement**: Place the reference character in the **"Present" of the Middle Layer**. Grasping a railing or pipe with one hand, pointing to the light of the upper layer with the other. Gaze **looking down on the past (bottom layer) while looking up at the future (top layer)**.
* **Tone**: Dynamic, tense. People's evacuation routes continue along the "wall roads."

### [Top Layer / Future and Conclusion] (PHASE 3: YEAR 2058)

* **Setting**: The "Stable Gravity Layer" above the clouds. City remnants are rearranged, forming a new residential area composed of white frame structures and transparent materials.
* **Description**: Ambiguous whether the gravity lattice was "repaired" or if a "new direction" was accepted. A pale column of light from the gravity well extends into the sky, connecting to the darkness below.
* **Tone**: Open and symbolic. Hope and unease coexist.

---

## ▼ Connection Points (Gimmicks connecting the layers: Required)

* **Gravity Well Light Column**: A column of pale blue volumetric light penetrating from the core of the bottom layer to the top layer. Particles flow upward.
* **Inverted Spiral Staircase**: "Up" and "down" switch midway. Small year plates on the staircase railing.
* **Waterfall where Falling Turns into Rising**: The water flow reverses at the boundary, emphasizing the layer separation.

---

## ▼ Visual Elements (Motifs to emphasize)

* Wet asphalt, trajectory of raindrops, neon reflections, fog, volumetric light.
* Curved City: Roads, architecture, and signs bend as a **continuous surface**, forming a **physical loop**.
* Small objects floating in the air (umbrellas, paper scraps, tools, petals) visualize the disturbance in the direction of gravity.
* Material changes in each layer: Rough rock stratum underground → Wet concrete and metal in the present → White structure and transparent material in the future.

---

## ▼ Text Instructions (On-screen Typography)

* **Title**: "**{argument name="Title" default="GRAVITY BROKEN TOKYO"}**"

  * Placement: **Sky of the Top Layer**, or engraved on the pedestal of the bottom layer.
  * Font: Inorganic sans-serif, movie poster style.
* **Famous Line** (Engrave on layer boundaries):

  * `{argument name="Famous Line" default=""Where is down, when the world forgets gravity?""}`
  * Placement: **Boundary between Middle and Top Layers**, like a sculpture on the landing of the inverted staircase or the edge of the light column.
* **Timeline Display (Optional)**: Small next to each layer

  * `PHASE 1 / YEAR 2036`
  * `PHASE 2 / YEAR 2046`
  * `PHAS
```

## 261. Split-Era Urban Diorama Generation Prompt for Nano Banana Pro

- **id**：4336
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=4336

### 描述

This is a highly detailed, multi-section system instruction designed for the Nano Banana Pro model to generate a photorealistic, isometric macro render of a split-era urban diorama. The prompt focuses on achieving Historical and Municipal Accuracy by contrasting two different time periods within the same city block, specifically detailing architectural styles, urban clutter, lighting, and composition for a realistic comparison.

### 示例图

![Split-Era Urban Diorama Generation Prompt for Nano Banana Pro](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1767508402926_ytdczy_G9jBsr8WUAEO1-f.jpg)

### 提示词

```
Do this for {argument name="City/Year A vs. City/Year B" default="New York 1700 vs New York 2025"}

<instructions>
Input Variable: [City/Year A vs. City/Year B] (e.g., New York 1900 vs. New York 2025)

System Instruction:
Generate a photorealistic, isometric macro render of a split-era urban diorama. The goal is Historical and Municipal Accuracy, avoiding sci-fi tropes or idealization.

1. Temporal Forensic Analysis:
Analyze the Dates:
 Strictly adhere to the architectural technologies available in Year A versus Year B.
The Left Block (The Past): Construct the Left side using the dominant vernacular of the era.
For 1900: Beaux-Arts stone, brick tenements, water towers, external fire escapes, and cobblestone/early asphalt.
The Right Block (The Present/Future): Construct the Right side using current urban realities, not futuristic fantasies.
For 2025: Prioritize "Super-Slender" Pencil Towers (Billionaire's Row style), Glass Curtain Walls mixed with preserved older facades, and "Hudson Yards" style angled steel.
  Crucial Detail: If the year is modern (2020+), you MUST include Urban Clutter: Green construction plywood barriers, sidewalk scaffolding (sheds), steam vents, and delivery trucks.

2. The Container (The Cross-Section):
The Base: Two suspended chunks of city infrastructure, revealing the "Underground" layer on the side profile.
Left Underground: Brick sewers and early subway tunnels.
Right Underground: Concrete conduit banks, fiber optic lines, and modern subway tiles.
The Divider: A jagged timeline fracture. No glowing sci-fi energy. The divide is a stark cut in the asphalt.

3. The Composition (The Density):
Left Street Life: Period-accurate transport (Horse-drawn carriages mixed with early Model T Fords or Trolleys). Pedestrians in dark coats/hats.
Right Street Life: Municipal Realism. Yellow Taxis, Amazon delivery vans, Uber cars, and bike lanes with cyclists. The street level should feel congested and chaotic.
Vegetation: Left has Elm trees (if applicable); Right has manicured "Corporate Plaza" planters and rooftop gardens.

4. Accurate Lighting Simulation (No "Neon Filters"):
Left Lighting: Carbon-Arc and Gaslight. The light should be dim, patchy, and warm (2400K), casting long shadows.
 Right Lighting: Mixed Municipal Lighting. A chaotic mix of warm sodium streetlights (orange), harsh white LED construction lights (6000K), and internal office lighting (neutral). Do not use "Cyberpunk Blue" or "Purple" unless it is a specific billboard.
Atmosphere: The Left is hazy from coal smoke. The Right has a clearer, sharper air quality but with light pollution.

5. Visual Syntax:
Camera: Isometric Orthographic projection, 45-degree angle.
Render Style: Photorealistic Architectural Visualization (ArchViz), 8k, V-Ray Render. Focus on texture fidelity (rust, wet pavement, grime) rather than bloom effects.

Output: 1:1 Aspect Ratio, Extreme Detail, Historically Authentic.

{

## 268. Magic Prompt for Automatic Infographic Generation with Nano Banana Pro

- **id**：4150
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=4150

### 描述

This prompt is designed for Nano Banana Pro, allowing users to input an entire article or report and automatically generate Napkin AI-quality infographics or visual summaries. It acts as a powerful tool for visual content creation from long-form text.

### 示例图

![Magic Prompt for Automatic Infographic Generation with Nano Banana Pro](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1767455045745_s83lhl_G9clYFEWoAAgGDo.jpg)

### 提示词

```
You are an expert in infographics. Read the article or report provided by the user and automatically generate a visually clear and attractive infographic that summarizes its content. Specifically, please create it considering the following elements:

### Objective
To visualize the main message, key data, process, or structure of the article or report so that it can be understood at a glance.

### Style and Tone
*   **Style**: A hand-drawn or minimal and sophisticated diagram style, similar to Napkin AI.
*   **Tone**: Professional yet approachable and visually appealing.

### Components
1.  **Title**: An attractive title that concisely represents the article's theme.
2.  **Key Points**: 3 to 5 of the most important points extracted from the entire article.
3.  **Diagrams**: Graphs, flowcharts, icons, or conceptual diagrams to represent the key points or processes.
4.  **Data**: If there are specific numbers or statistical data in the article, display them in an emphasized manner.
5.  **Conclusion/Action**: A summary of the article or the next action expected from the reader.

### Output Format
Based on the content of the provided article, output the overall image of the infographic, integrating the above components, as detailed instructions. Describe the diagram ideas, layout suggestions, and the image of the colors and fonts to be used specifically.

### Article/Report
{argument name="Content of the Article or Report" default="[Paste the full text of the article or report here]"}
```

## 269. Historical Archivist Prompt for Flawed Inventions

- **id**：4079
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=4079

### 描述

A complex, multi-phase prompt designed for the AI to act as a Historical Archivist, generating a photorealistic macro photograph of a container overflowing with the physical forms of four obscure, failed historical prototypes from a user-specified era, complete with legible, handwritten labels detailing the invention and its fatal flaw.

### 示例图

![Historical Archivist Prompt for Flawed Inventions](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1767454930384_i85347_G9TrT6_WQAE5a3t.jpg)

![Historical Archivist Prompt for Flawed Inventions](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1767454930718_xcrera_G9Trm2aXIAAcNTP.jpg)

### 提示词

```
Role: Historical Archivist & Technical Illustrator.
Input Era/Region: {argument name="era and region" default="US, 1820s"}

Phase 1: The Deep Dive (AI to Deduce)
1.  Identify Container: Era-appropriate storage (Chest, Cabinet, Box).
2.  Identify Paperwork: Era-appropriate medium (Vellum, Blueprints, Floppy Disks).
3.  Identify 4 Obscure Failures:
    Constraint: Do NOT select famous successes. Select Bizarre, Failed, or Dangerous prototypes.
   Data Extraction: For each item, identify its Historical Name and its Fatal Flaw.

Phase 2: The Visual Translation Layer (Geometry)
Logic: The AI must explicitly analyze the Physical Form Factor of each selected invention.
Instruction: Do not render generic shapes. If it's a "Steam Man," render a brass robot. If it's a "Pneumatic Tube," render the piping.

Phase 3: Visual Execution
Subject: The [Container] overflowing with the [Signature Geometries] of the lost inventions.

1. The "Lost" Prototypes:
Render the 4 inventions based strictly on their Physical Form Factor.
Material: Authentic materials (Brass, Wood, Bakelite, Clay) based on the era.

2. The Labels (CRITICAL UPDATE):
Format: Each item has a clearly attached tag (String/Wax/Adhesive) with Legible Text.
Text Structure:
Line 1:[The Invention Name] (e.g., "The Dynasphere").
Line 2:[The Flaw] (e.g., "Unstable Steering").
Note:Ensure the text looks handwritten or stamped according to the time period.

3. The Narrative:
The Inventors: Tiny figurines reacting to the failure (frustrated, crying, kicking the machine).
The Contrast: A generic Modern Smartphone (or futuristic device) sitting on the edge for scale/irony.

4. Atmosphere:
Lighting: Dramatic, moody, specific to the era.
Texture: Macro details of rust, dust, and age.

Output: ONE image, 4:5, Photorealistic Macro Photography, Legible Text Labels.
```

- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=4155

### 描述

This tweet showcases the power of Nano-banana Pro, an AI tool, to instantly generate a detailed persona. The prompt is designed to create a comprehensive profile for a target audience member, including demographics, psychographics, needs, and pain points, which is highly useful for marketing, content creation, and product development.

### 示例图

![Nano-banana Pro Persona Generation Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1767455052602_plknk6_G9Y3axwakAA5VH5.jpg)

### 提示词

```
You are a professional persona designer. Based on the following information, please design a detailed persona.

**Target Audience:** {argument name="target audience" default="Women in their 30s, living in the city center, working at an IT company"}
**Objective:** {argument name="objective" default="Developing a marketing strategy for a new beauty serum"}

**Elements to include in the persona:**
1. Basic information (Name, Age, Occupation, Residence, Family structure)
2. Personality and Values
3. Lifestyle (Daily routine, Hobbies, Information sources)
4. Challenges and Needs (Beauty concerns, Desired solutions)
5. Goals and Motivation
6. Purchasing behavior (Points of emphasis, Budget range, Purchase channels)
7. Touchpoints with the service/product (Usage scenarios, Expected effects)

**Output format:**
- Use appropriate headings for an easily readable structure.
- Include specific descriptions that could be used for a persona image.
```

## 277. Miniature Bridge of Ancient Mathematical Knowledge

- **id**：3992
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=3992

### 描述

A complex instruction prompt for generating a mythological epic image visualizing the transmission of an invention or technology (using 'math' as the input) across ancient civilizations. The output should be a miniature bridge structure connecting 3-5 civilization 'islands' on an aged parchment map, featuring distinct architectural styles, tiny figurines, and golden hour lighting.

### 示例图

![Miniature Bridge of Ancient Mathematical Knowledge](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1767166942003_7vuk4n_G9TRRL0XAAATXHv.jpg)

### 提示词

```
<instruction>
Input A is an invention or technology  
Analyze: how this innovation appeared independently or was transmitted across ancient civilizations (Egyptian, Chinese, Islamic, Mayan, Greek, etc.).
Goal: Miniature bridge structure connecting 3-5 civilization "islands."
Rules:
* Base: ancient world map (aged parchment) as foundation
* Bridge structure: ornate multi-level walkway with different architectural styles for each section (columns, arches, pagoda elements)
* Each "island" platform: distinct civilization's version of Input A invention (Chinese compass vs Viking sunstone, Mayan zero vs Indian zero, etc.)
* Civilization figurines: tiny scholars/inventors from each culture examining each other's versions
* Include: trade route markers, tiny ships/caravans between islands, scrolls being passed
* Center of bridge: the "universal truth" they all discovered (mathematical formula, physical principle) etched in neutral symbol
* Materials: stone textures, gold leaf accents, cultural pattern details
* Lighting: mystical golden hour with light ray connecting all islands
Output: 1 x image, 16:9 landscape for maximum bridge span visibility, mythological epic photography
</instruction>
```

The style utilizes a modular Bento Grid layout, clean medical UI design, and a specific clinical color palette to present complex information like pharmacology, usage, and precautions.

```
{
"image_analysis": {
"subject": "{argument name="drug name" default="Semaglutide"} Science Infographic, 9:16",
"style": "Modular Bento Grid Design / Minimalist Medical UI Design / Approachable Feel",
"tone": "Professional, Clinical, Rigorous, Scientific, Trustworthy, Humanized, Easy-to-read",
"primary_colors": ["Clinical White", "Medical Blue", "Slate Grey", "Mint Green"]
},
"prompt_elements": {
"subject_description": "A high-quality science infographic poster about [Drug Name], designed to combine professional rigor with humanized care.",
"layout": "Bento box style modular grid layout, clean medical UI/UX interface design, divided into clear rectangular cards with soft edges, providing a comfortable visual experience.",
"visual_details": [
"Top header with a striking modern sans-serif font title 'Drug Chinese Name' and its chemical/generic name. A gentle medical icon (like a heart or green leaf) can be added next to the title.",
"Center top features a hyper-realistic macro photograph of the [Drug Dosage Form/Packaging] (e.g., tablet, capsule, or vial), with soft lighting, appearing safe and reliable.",

"Part 5: Precautions grid with simple warning icons ([Drug Interactions], [Contraindications], [Side Effects]), icons should gently remind rather than cause panic, text descriptions should be concise and highlight key points.",

"Part 7: Drug storage guidelines with thermometer and humidity icons, icons designed to be simple and clear, text descriptions easy to understand."
],
"art_style": "Minimalist scientific visualization design, combining detailed medical illustrations with product photography, flat medical icons, Swiss style typography, focusing on readability and approachability.",

"color_palette": "Monochromatic clinical white or light gray background, translucent frosted glass cards, dark gray and navy blue text, accented with medical blue or cyan to highlight key points, the overall color scheme gives a fresh, comfortable feeling."
},

### 描述

A detailed prompt designed to visualize a user's annual music report (like NetEase Cloud Music's) as an ultra-realistic, high-end transparent acrylic vinyl record spinning on a classic turntable. The prompt specifies the integration of data (listening hours, top artists, genres) directly onto the transparent record surface using elegant, modern fonts and neon glow effects, set against a heavily blurred, dreamy starry background.

### 示例图

![Annual Music Report Vinyl Record Infographic Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1767167000877_8ohnfb_G9VrQ91bsAAYlZl.jpg)

### 提示词

```
Hyper-realistic professional photography, a high-end transparent acrylic vinyl record placed on a classic turntable, slowly rotating and playing. The overall scene is set on a warm wooden turntable stand, with a dark, dreamy starry sky mixed with gradient neon lights in the background, creating a retro yet modern atmosphere. Subject - Transparent Acrylic Vinyl Record and Turntable: Record Material: Crystal clear transparent acrylic, with realistic reflections, glowing edges, and refraction effects. Classic vinyl groove textures are visible on the surface, but the whole is crystal clear. The record is rotating, with a slight motion blur.
Turntable: Retro style wooden or metal platter base, the tonearm gently rests on the record, the stylus touches the grooves, details are realistic, including the spindle and speed adjustment knob.
Content Style: Elegant modern typography combined with a slight hand-drawn feel. Text is directly “engraved” or projected onto the transparent record surface, with slightly imperfect lines.
Record Center Label Area: Circular label printed with NetEase Cloud Music's red LOGO, surrounded by the words "{argument name="report year" default="2025"} Annual Listening Report." The label rotates with the record.

Content Layout (Text and elements cleverly integrated into the record surface and grooves): Top Area (Upper outer edge of the record): Large title: "Your {argument name="report year" default="2025"} Annual Listening Report" using bold modern font, with purple-blue gradient and neon purple edge glow.

Center Area - Favorite Artist Ranking (occupying about 40% of the center): Central illustration: Hand-drawn style reinforcement of the center label with the iconic pattern of #1 {argument name="artist 1" default="HuiChunDan"} (rainbow gradient circle), surrounded by small avatar icons of artists like Taylor Swift and Li Ronghao, slightly dynamic with rotation. Avatar icons are sourced from the reference image.
Art Style: Simple line art with slight hand-drawn imperfections, full of personality.
Colors: Main fill in deep purple and blue tones, white outline, neon purple and orange highlights.
Artist ranking text arranged along the record grooves: #1 {argument name="artist 1" default="HuiChunDan"} 497 times, #2 Taylor Swift 493 times, #3 Li Ronghao 420 times, #4 Ma Yuan Shi Ren 327 times (Top 4), elegant glowing font, curved along the groove lines.

Surrounding Information Display (Circular layout, text and icons distributed on the outer ring of the record and the turntable panel): Left Side (Left panel of the turntable) - Total Listening Volume and Style: Icons: Music note and heart.
“You listened to 2839 songs this year”
“New exploration songs accounted for 68%”
“Cyan is the average color of the music world this year”
Hand-drawn small arrow pointing to the center of the record.

Top Right (Space above the record) - Annual Genre TOP 5: Icon: Flowing waveform.
“Your {argument name="report year" default="2025"} Annual Genre TOP 5”
European/American Pop 49%, Cantonese Pop 15%, Pop Rock 15%, Indie Rock 12%, Alternative/Indie 9%
Displayed using percentage circles or sector diagrams surrounding the outer edge of the record, slightly glowing.

Bottom (Turntable base panel) - Listening Duration: Icons: Hourglass and play button.
“Your listening duration this year is 695 hours and 25 minutes”
“Exceeding 95.2% of users”
“Your listening duration is the absolute authority”

Connecting Elements: Hand-drawn arrows and dashed lines connecting various parts of the turntable to the rotating record.
Small Decorative Elements: Scattered music notes, stars, light spots, NetEase Cloud red small clouds, slightly floating with rotation.
All text is in Chinese, elegant modern font with a slight handwritten feel.

Color Scheme: Baseline: Cream white texture (#F5F5DC)
Main Illustration: Deep purple-blue gradient fill, white outline
Highlights: Neon purple (#A100FF) and orange used for emphasis and decoration
Icons and Charts: Light grey (#B0B0B0) line art
Brand Color: NetEase Cloud red (#E60026) used for LOGO and key emphasis

Bottom Area (Front edge of the turntable): Dark gradient horizontal bar background
Left: NetEase Cloud Music Red LOGO (metallic glow)
Center: “NetEase Cloud Music” standard text
Right: Small text “Open NetEase Cloud Music Search 【Annual Report】”

Environment and Photography: Background: Dark dreamy starry sky with purple-blue neon gradient, extremely heavy blur.
Depth of Field: f/1.4 large aperture simulation - background is almost completely abstract.
Only soft purple, blue, and pink bokeh light spots are visible, with no discernible shapes.
Perspective: Slightly overhead 45-degree frontal view, record and turntable perfectly centered, capturing rotational dynamics.
Lighting: Warm side light and professional spotlight creating strong reflections on the acrylic surface, dramatic neon purple glowing edge, subtle highlights captured on the tonearm and stylus.
Atmosphere: Nighttime retro dreamy atmosphere, transparent acrylic record glowing while spinning on the turntable, heavy background blur highlighting the main subject.

Technical Specifications: 8K ultra-high resolution, cinematic color grading high contrast, realistic acrylic material physics (edge glow, reflection, refraction, slight rotational blur), exquisite modern text details, high-end music photography quality, extreme background bokeh making the record and turntable content the absolute focal point. Key requirement: Background must be extremely blurred (almost abstract bokeh), the central transparent vinyl record must have a realistic sense of rotation, elegant modern text integrated into the record surface, all elements incorporating the user-provided 2025 NetEase Cloud annual summary data, strong overall retro turntable atmosphere.
```

## 285. Macro Diorama Historical Rivalry (Science Wars)

- **id**：3929
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=3929

### 描述

A complex, multi-phase prompt designed to generate a hyper-detailed macro diorama image illustrating a historical or modern rivalry (e.g., Elon Musk vs. a competitor). It requires integrating era-appropriate documents (the 'stage'), tiny figurines of the rivals in period clothing, giant tools, and critically, legible environmental text (identity anchors) to label the figures without floating text. This prompt is highly conceptual and structured.

### 示例图

![Macro Diorama Historical Rivalry (Science Wars)](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1767166866151_b0xdhd_G9B-1KFWIAAuVhM.jpg)

![Macro Diorama Historical Rivalry (Science Wars)](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1767166866879_kr768n_G9B-1KFXMAA8otj.jpg)

### 提示词

```
<instructions> 
Input: Invention/Rivalry = {argument name="invention/rivalry" default="[User Choice]"}

Phase 1: Chronological Profiling
Ancient (Pre-500 AD): Stone/Papyrus, Oil Lamps. Labeling = Carved symbols or scroll signatures.
Renaissance (1400-1600): Parchment, Candlelight. Labeling = Wax seals, hand-lettered envelopes, book spines.
Industrial (1800-1940): Blueprints, Gaslight. Labeling = Typed Patent headers, Business Cards, Newspaper Headlines.
Modern (1950-Present): Circuit diagrams, Neon. Labeling = ID Badges, Magazine covers, Computer login screens.

Phase 2:
Goal: A hyper-detailed Macro Diorama where the environment identifies the rivals.

1. The Stage (The Document):
 The ground is the primary design medium of the era (Clay Tablet, Parchment, Blueprint, or CAD Printout).
The Invention:A 3D Prototype is rising from the center, separating the two figures.

2. The Living Miniatures: Two tiny, hyper-realistic figurines (The Rivals) interact with the invention.
  They are dressed strictly in period-accurate clothing.

3. The Identity Anchors (CRITICAL - Labeling):
Instruction: You must seamlessly integrate readable text into the "Giant" props near each figure to identify them to the viewer.
Prop A (Near Rival 1): Place a "Giant" personal item with their name visible. (e.g., A letter addressed to "Mr. T. Edison", a book titled "By C. Darwin", or a security badge reading "S. Jobs").
Prop B (Near Rival 2):Do the same for the second figure. (e.g., A Patent application signed "Nikola Tesla", or a court summons for "Mark Zuckerberg").
Context: These items must look like they were carelessly left on the desk, not floating labels.

4. The "Giant" Atmosphere:
The Tools: Giant-scale era-appropriate tools (Chisels, Quills, Soldering Irons) tower over the figures.
The Clutter: Period-specific debris (Marble dust, Ink spills, Burnt fuses, Empty soda cans).

5. Technical Specs:
Lighting: Era-appropriate source (Fire vs. Filament vs. LED).
Perspective:Tilt-shift macro photography (45-degree angle). focus on the miniatures.

Output: ONE image, 4:5 aspect ratio, photorealistic historical macro photography with legible environmental text. </instructions>
```

## 291. Historical Macro Diorama: Culinary Atlas Grid

- **id**：3540
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=3540

### 描述

A complex, multi-phase prompt designed to generate a 2x2 grid of 'Historical Macro Dioramas' based on user-selected ingredients. It requires the AI to first determine an 'Era Texture' (Ancient, Renaissance, Industrial, Modern) and then create a hyper-detailed macro photograph of a messy workspace. Each panel features an open book with 2D sketches and a 3D pop-up diorama of a finished meal, complete with tiny period-accurate figurines of an inventor and a rival.

### 示例图

![Historical Macro Diorama: Culinary Atlas Grid](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1766987648345_zwo194_G9B-BdaXoAAdW5M.jpg)

![Historical Macro Diorama: Culinary Atlas Grid](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1766987648285_yidarh_G9B-BdSXAAAbtMq.jpg)

### 提示词

```
Role:Historical Art Director & Macro Photographer.
Input: Invention or Rivalry = [{argument name="user choice" default="User Choice"}]

Phase 1: Chronological Profiling
Before rendering, analyze the Year of the invention to determine the "Era Texture":
If Ancient (e.g., 200 BC): Surface = Rough Stone or Papyrus. Light = Oil Lamp/Torch. Clutter = Clay shards, bronze chisels, scrolls, figs/grapes.
If Renaissance (e.g., 1500): Surface = Dark Oak & Vellum. Light = Candle. Clutter = Quill feathers, wax seals, brass astrolabes, leather binding.
If Industrial (e.g., 1890): Surface = Green Felt or Mahogany. Light = Gas/Filament. Clutter = Newsprint, pocket watches, copper wire, soot.
If Modern (e.g., 1980): Surface = Formica or Metal. Light = Fluorescent/Neon. Clutter = Circuit boards, Polaroid photos, soda cans, plastic casings.

Phase 2: Visual Execution
Goal: A hyper-detailed "Macro Diorama" where the environment tells the story of the time period.
Subject: A messy, chaotic workspace viewed through a macro lens.

1. The Stage (The Document):
The "ground" is the primary design medium of that era 
The surface is filled with era-specific sketches/carvings of the invention.

2. The Living Miniatures:
Two tiny, hyper-realistic figurines (The Inventor & The Rival) stand on this surface.
They are dressed strictly in period-accurate clothing (Togas vs. Frock Coats vs. Turtlenecks).
Action: They are interacting with a 3D Prototype of the invention that is rising physically out of the document/tablet.

3. The "Giant" Atmosphere (Dynamic Enrichment):
The Wall: A massive background object establishing context (e.g., a carved stone stele, a tapestried wall, a vintage newspaper, or a stack of VHS tapes).
The Tools: Giant-scale tools used to make the invention loom over the figures like trees (e.g., a giant chisel, a massive quill dipped in ink, or a soldering iron).
The Debris: The mess of the creative process (e.g., stone dust, spilled ink, burnt fuses, or crumpled layout paper).
The Comforts: Food/Drink of the era (e.g., an earthen jug of wine, a porcelain tea cup, or a can of cola).

4. Technical Specs:
Lighting: Must match the "Era Light" determined in Phase 1 (Flickering warm fire vs. cold electric buzz).
Perspective: Tilt-shift macro photography (45-degree angle).
Texture: Extreme focus on material weathering (rust, dust, parchment cracks, or plastic sheen).

Output: ONE image, 4:5 aspect ratio, "Historical Macro Realism."
```

## 295. Historical Scientist Rivalry Macro Diorama Prompt

- **id**：3684
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=3684

### 描述

A complex, multi-phase prompt for generating a hyper-realistic macro diorama image showing two historical scientific rivals (e.g., Einstein and a competitor) as tiny figurines battling over an invention on a giant patent diagram, emphasizing cinematic lighting and period-accurate clutter.

### 示例图

![Historical Scientist Rivalry Macro Diorama Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1766988020871_9b1u2c_G9B79KwW8AAM_W0.jpg)

### 提示词

```
Do this for {argument name="scientist 1" default="Einstein"} and a competitor of his 

<instructions>
 Invention = [{argument name="invention" default="Electricity"}]

Phase 1: Historical Conflict Analysis
1.  Identify Rivals: Determine the two main historical figures associated with this invention (e.g., Tesla vs. Edison, Wright Bros vs. Curtiss).
2.  Identify The Tech: Distinguish their competing methods (e.g., AC Coils vs. DC Bulbs).
3.  Identify The Era: Select appropriate "Giant" background clutter (e.g., Victorian newspapers, 1920s fountain pens).

Phase 2: Visual Execution
Goal: A "Macro Diorama" of a Rivalry. A messy, highly detailed flat-lay of a shared historical workspace where the two rivals exist as tiny living figurines battling over their creation.

Rules:
The Stage: A large, yellowed Patent Diagram or Blueprint lies flat on a wooden desk. The paper is wrinkled and covered in scribbled corrections.

The Tiny Rivals: Two hyper-realistic miniature figurines (Inventor A and Rival B) are standing on opposite sides of the document. They are positioned at their own tiny wooden desks or workstations on top of the paper.

The Conflict: They are gesturing aggressively at each other. The Invention itself is physically emerging from the center of the paper between them (e.g., a glowing 3D lightbulb or a spinning biplane propeller), acting as the barrier or prize they are fighting over.

The "Giant" Terrain (Surroundings): The scene is surrounded by massive, period-accurate objects acting as a landscape:

A "Giant" wall of newspapers with headlines about their legal battles.
 Massive burnt fuses or broken prototypes scattered like boulders.
 A spilled cup of coffee creating a "lake" near one of the figures.
  Giant tools (calipers, screwdrivers) casting long shadows.
Perspective: Slightly angled 45-degree macro shot looking down at the patent.
Texture: Dust motes, scratched mahogany wood, paper grain, metallic reflections.
Lighting: Cinematic Macro Lighting. A warm desk lamp illuminates the scene, but the Invention in the center emits its own light (e.g., blue electrical arcs vs. yellow filament glow), lighting the faces of the tiny figures.

Output: ONE image, 4:5 aspect ratio, photorealistic historical macro photography, tilt-shift effect.
</instructions>
```

## 298. Historical Ingredient Journey Diorama

- **id**：3432
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=3432

### 描述

A complex, logic-based prompt designed to create a macro diorama showing the historical trade journey of a specific ingredient. The prompt requires the AI to analyze the ingredient's Golden Age of trade, select period-accurate containers, maps, transport vehicles (like a steam train or camel), and dress figurines in period attire, culminating in a preserved specimen of the ingredient at the center.

### 示例图

![Historical Ingredient Journey Diorama](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1766940082954_uv5t7w_G85AR55agAASK_B.jpg)

![Historical Ingredient Journey Diorama](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1766940085201_dz9ps3_G85AR5cXUAAVVSQ.jpg)

### 提示词

```
<instructions>Input Variable: {argument name="ingredient" default="INSERT INGREDIENT"}

 Logic 1. Analyze the Era: Before generating, determine the Golden Age of trade for the [Input]. (Example: Beef = 1880s Industrial Rail; Spices = 1500s Silk Road; Bananas = 1920s Steamships). 

2. The Container: The chest material must match the Era. If Industrial: Rusted steel, riveted iron, or branded shipping crate wood. If Ancient: Carved sandalwood, brass-bound oak, or woven reed. If Modern: Polymer, aluminum, or sterile medical glass. 

3. The Map (Inner Lid): Style must match the Era. (Blueprints for industrial, parchment for ancient, nautical charts for maritime). Route must be accurate. (Rail lines for beef, trade winds for fruit, caravan trails for spices). 

4. The Diorama (Base): Transport: Use only the historically correct vehicle. (Steam train, Cargo ship, Camel, Truck, Ox-cart). Figures: Dress figurines in period-correct attire. (Cowboys/Butchers, Sailors/Dockworkers, Nomads/Merchants). Setting: The background of the box interior must match the source origin. (Stockyards, Tropical Port, Desert Oasis).

 5. The Specimen (Center): The ingredient is the "Jewel." Encased in a preservation method distinct to the item. (Block of unmelting ice for meat, vacuum tube for delicate organics, crystal phial for powders, wax seal for liquids). Visual Output Rules: Macro lens, shallow depth of field. Volumetric lighting appropriate to the setting (e.g. sunlight, Lantern). No text overlays. Aspect Ratio 1:1.</instructions>
```

A detailed prompt for generating a museum-grade presentation board about a supermassive black hole (Sagittarius A*). The composition is split into three sections: scientific diagrams (left), an exploded axonometric view (center), and a photorealistic 3D render (right), transitioning from archival blueprint tones to cosmic colors.

![Supermassive Black Hole Museum Presentation Board](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1766940103221_4is4i5_G8_At_SXUAAqDPb.jpg)

![Supermassive Black Hole Museum Presentation Board](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1766940103261_36s00r_G8_At_dWUAEShHj.jpg)

### 提示词

```
An expert astronomer's museum-grade presentation board for a supermassive black hole — event horizon, glowing accretion disk, relativistic jets. Left: black-and-white 2D scientific diagrams (cross-section, orbital paths, spectral analysis) with precise annotations, scale bars, and technical labels. Center: exploded axonometric or layered diagram revealing singularity core, plasma torus, and spacetime curvature layers. Right: photorealistic 3D render of the black hole in the galactic center with swirling stars and distorted light, vibrant orange and blue plasma glow, stellar debris trails. Visual style transitions from archival blueprint tones (graphite schematics, orbital precision) to cosmic atmospheric palette (deep blacks, fiery oranges, electric blues). ## 308. Surreal Abstract Concept Contained in a Display Case

- **id**：3263
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=3263

### 描述

A highly detailed, conceptual prompt for Nano Banana Pro to generate a hyper-detailed, surreal studio scene. The image features an abstract concept physically contained within a transparent, museum-grade display case, where the subject is partially transformed into a contrasting material (e.g., skin turning to stone), emphasizing frozen motion, scientific annotation, and a quiet, contemplative mood.

### 示例图

![Surreal Abstract Concept Contained in a Display Case](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1766673346119_d07q50_G84SOava0AAmmHU.jpg)

![Surreal Abstract Concept Contained in a Display Case](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1766673347214_hj7uaf_G84SOarakAACwfq.jpg)

![Surreal Abstract Concept Contained in a Display Case](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1766673349052_g7ull9_G84SOatbwAAmuS6.jpg)

![Surreal Abstract Concept Contained in a Display Case](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1766673352543_kw7hu1_G84SOataIAAZ7qG.jpg)

### 提示词

```
A hyper-detailed, surreal but believable studio scene where an abstract concept is physically contained and made visible.

Main structure:
A transparent, museum-grade display container (thick glass or acrylic), placed alone in a neutral studio space. Inside the container is a frozen moment in time.

Core subject:
A human-scale subject (person or symbolic object) partially transformed into another material that contradicts its original nature — for example: {argument name="transformation example" default="skin turning into stone, soft fabric crystallizing, organic elements embedded in mechanical structures"}. The transformation is incomplete, showing a clear boundary between “before” and “after”.

Conceptual twist:
The interior of the container is treated like a scientific or archival exhibit:
– clearly layered composition
– visible cross-sections or cutaway logic
– subtle measurement marks, labels, or annotation-style details
– the feeling that this object is being studied, preserved, or explained

Spatial logic:
Gravity behaves normally, but time does not — motion is implied yet completely frozen.
Small secondary elements ({argument name="secondary elements" default="dust, droplets, fragments, threads, petals, gears, etc."}) are suspended mid-air, reinforcing the sense of paused time.

Lighting:
Soft, directional studio lighting with strong material definition.
Light emphasizes texture differences (smooth vs rough, organic vs artificial).
No dramatic colors — restrained, high-end, museum-like color palette.

Camera & composition:
Straight-on or slightly elevated perspective.
Centered composition, calm framing.
Depth comes from internal layers, not camera tricks.
Photorealistic, macro-level detail, cinematic clarity.

Mood:
Quiet, unsettling, contemplative.
The image should feel intelligent rather than emotional, poetic rather than decorative.

Style constraints:
No fantasy clichés.
No obvious sci-fi UI.
No decorative surrealism.
Everything should feel physically possible, even if conceptually strange.

Ultra high resolution, extremely sharp details, realistic materials, controlled studio background, premium art photography aesthetic.
```

## 309. Tarot Celtic Cross Spread Reading Generation Prompt

- **id**：3279
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=3279

### 描述

A highly complex, technical prompt for Nano Banana Pro designed to generate a visual representation and interpretation of a Celtic Cross Tarot spread. It specifies canvas settings, background design (mystical dark purple/gold), detailed card rendering (78 standard cards, gold metallic vines, Japanese names), the 10-card spread positions, and a detailed text layer for interpretation and meaning.

### 示例图

![Tarot Celtic Cross Spread Reading Generation Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1766673393152_xcea5d_G82rB6sagAMf3co.jpg)

![Tarot Celtic Cross Spread Reading Generation Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1766673395268_o9xirl_G82rB6CaUAAzwMy.jpg)

### 提示词

```
# Generate Results for the Celtic Cross Spread
## Fortune-Telling Parameters
- Card indicating the essence of the problem: {argument name="card essence of problem" default="(Fill in 1 card from 78 cards)"}
- Question to be asked: {argument name="question to ask" default="(Describe in one sentence)"}
## Image Requirements
### Canvas Settings
- 2048*2048px 2K image
- Cards and text elements must be at least 20px away from the canvas edges
- The card layer should be placed horizontally centered and in the upper 2/3 vertically
- The text layer (explanation section) should be placed in the lower 1/3 vertically
### Background Layer
- Dark purple background with dark gold stars, clouds, moon, sun, and lines of circles and triangles added to create a mystical atmosphere
### Card Layer
- Use all 78 standard Tarot cards. Breakdown: 22 Major Arcana (from "The Fool" to "The World") and 56 Minor Arcana (4 suits: Wands, Cups, Swords, Pentacles).
- Each Minor Arcana suit includes number cards 1 through 10 and 4 Court cards.
- The edges of the cards are framed with golden metallic vines, and the inside depicts the corresponding image and the name in Japanese. The size of each card is unified and arranged so as not to be cramped. To ensure the text and images of the overlapped cards are visible, the short side (bottom and top edges) of the card should be 1/2 the length of the long side.
- Each extracted card must appear only once. If duplication occurs during random extraction, redraw a different card.
- Exclude the card specified in "Fortune-Telling Parameters - Card indicating the essence of the problem," and extract the spread from the remaining cards.
- Extract 1 card randomly, placed vertically in the center.
- Extract 1 card randomly, placed horizontally in the center (with the top of the card facing left), overlapping the vertical center card to form a cross.
- Extract 1 card randomly for each position (Up, Down, Left, Right) surrounding the central cross.
- Extract 4 cards randomly in a vertical column on the right side.
### Meaning of the Spread
- Center, vertical: Current situation and personality
- Center, horizontal: Obstacles and contradictions
- Up: Hopes and achievements
- Down: Potential and influence
- Left: Past
- Right: Near future
- Right column, 1st card: Wishes and concerns
- Right column, 2nd card: Environment and family
- Right column, 3rd card: Result of the reading
- Right column, 4th card: Final outcome
### Text Layer
- Use Mincho font, dark gold color, with a 2px black border (#000).
- Card Name: Display the name in Japanese at the bottom center of each generated card image. For horizontally placed cards, the name should be displayed at the bottom of the card itself (not the bottom relative to the horizontal placement).
- Explanation Section: Include the following content in the explanation section.
   - On the left side of the explanation section, list all extracted cards in the format: "[Meaning of the Spread] Card Name".
   - On the right side of the explanation section, describe the possible interpretation by connecting the meaning of each card in the spread, based on the "Fortune-Telling Parameters - Question to be asked".
   - Decorate the explanation section with a golden vine pattern border.
```

## 312. 3D Clay Style Year-End Summary Poster Generator

- **id**：3116
- **需要参考图**：是
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=3116

### 描述

A highly detailed, structured prompt for generating a visually impactful 3D year-end summary poster (like a ChatGPT summary) based on an uploaded screenshot of user data. The style is fixed: 3D clay/soft plastic texture (like a 'blind box toy'), high saturation colors, and a clear, magazine-like layout. The core visual is a tightly packed clay sphere composed of data bubbles, themed icons, and miniature architectural elements representing chat categories.

### 示例图

![3D Clay Style Year-End Summary Poster Generator](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1766936028559_5kv8ud_G803VYoWEAAMilR.jpg)

### 提示词

```
Task Goal:
Based on the data and information in the year-end summary screenshot uploaded by the user, design a 3D year-end summary poster with a distinct style and strong visual impact, username {argument name="Username" default="[guizang]"}

Overall Style and Mood (Fixed Style):
Core Style: Blending 3D trends, gamification elements, and high-saturation colors.
Material Feel: The main body of the image presents exquisite clay or soft plastic material rendering, with rounded edges and soft light and shadow, giving a friendly 'blind box toy' feel.

Layout Sense: The overall layout should resemble a refined, high-end magazine, well-spaced, with clear information architecture. Text layout should be concise and clear, without adding any decorative backgrounds like cards, capsules, or bottom frames.

Main Visual (Core Visual):
The visual center is a massive, tightly stacked Q-version 3D clay sphere. The elements composing the sphere need to be dynamically generated based on the specific content in the user's uploaded screenshot:

Data Bubbles: Key numbers and corresponding labels extracted from the screenshot (e.g., total messages, chat times, images generated, activity ranking, etc.) are made into rounded clay data bubbles embedded in the sphere.

Theme Icons: Based on the user's interests reflected in the screenshot, add corresponding general clay icons (e.g., game controller, brain, chart, chat bubble, etc.).

Category Buildings (Core Requirement): Analyze the focus of the content in the screenshot and integrate several exquisite miniature clay buildings representing different chat categories into the sphere. For example:
If it includes AI/technology content, incorporate a futuristic data center building with a sense of technology.
If it includes gaming content, incorporate a pixel-style game castle.
If it includes creative/art content, incorporate a miniature art studio.
Combination Method: All these elements (bubbles, icons, buildings) must be very tightly and harmoniously squeezed together, seamlessly forming a perfect sphere, without revealing the internal structure.

Background Design (Fixed Frame):
Base Color: Use a high-saturation gradient background from warm orange to soft purple (referencing previous style).

Background Text: Behind the sphere, place six massive Chinese characters with splatter texture and reverse etching effects. The themes can be "Adventure Innovation Exploration" (top row) and "Creation Hardcore Impact" (bottom row), partially obscured by the sphere to add a street-style hardcore feel.

Information Architecture and Layout (Content Extracted from Screenshot):
Top (Header): Place the white ChatGPT Logo and generate a title based on the screenshot information, formatted as: "[{argument name="Username" default="Username extracted from screenshot"}]'s [Year] Year-End Summary".

Bottom Data Area (Data Grid): Below the sphere, create a minimalist 2x2 pure text data grid. Select the 4 most core data points from the screenshot, use an extra-bold sans-serif font to display the large numbers, and accompany them with corresponding text labels and interesting social attribute phrases (e.g., "Social Superstar," "Chatterbox," "Hexagonal Warrior," etc., generated based on data characteristics).
Footer Summary (Footer): Typeset two paragraphs of text in small font at the very bottom, with content extracted directly from the summary paragraphs in the user's screenshot (such as "Your Chat Style" and "Annual Highlights").
```

## 313. AI Agent Development 'Swamp' Diagram Prompt

- **id**：3132
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=3132

### 描述

A detailed JSON-based prompt for Nano Banana Pro to generate a 'Swamp' diagram (a popular internet meme format) illustrating the circular nature of getting stuck in AI Agent development. The prompt defines the diagram's structure, style (simple, cute editorial), layout (straight intro track leading into a clockwise loop), and specific nodes with characters, visuals, and dialogue/thought terms.

### 示例图

![AI Agent Development 'Swamp' Diagram Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1766667407596_i77nzi_G8ybmHWagAUO-CF.jpg)

### 提示词

```
diagram:
  title: "{argument name="diagram title" default="The Cycle of the Generative AI Agent Development Swamp"}"
  style:
    character: "simple_editorial_cute"
    tone: "light_like_original"
    line: "soft_round"
    shading: "flat_minimal"
    palette: "muted_editorial"
    background: "light_beige_or_white"

  rule:
    first_character_only: "Only the first person is different"
    others_same_character: "All others are the same person"
    anxiety_requires_tech_terms: true
    dialogue_tone: "Maintain the short, light phrasing of the original diagram"

layout:
  intro_track: "The upper section is a straight line (left to right), then drops down to join the loop"
  loop_track: "Arranged as a loop (ring), with arrows curving clockwise along the circumference"

nodes:
  # ===== Upper Section: Straight Introduction =====
  - id: A
    zone: "intro"
    position: "upper_left"
    actor: "Different person (Senior Engineer)"
    visual: "Pointing at a laptop"
    item: "AI Agent Demo Screen"
    says: "It'll make you happy."
    arrow_to: { id: B, shape: "straight", direction: "right" }

  - id: B
    zone: "intro"
    position: "upper_center"
    actor: "Protagonist (Same person)"
    visual: "Waving lightly"
    says: "Even a simple agent..."
    arrow_to: { id: C, shape: "straight", direction: "right" }

  - id: C
    zone: "intro"
    position: "upper_right"
    actor: "Protagonist (Same person)"
    visual: "Executing sample code"
    item: "LangChain / CrewAI Sample"
    arrow_to: { id: D, shape: "straight", direction: "down" }

  # ===== D: Awakening Point =====
  - id: D
    zone: "connector"
    position: "right_outer_before_loop"
    actor: "Protagonist (Same person)"
    visual:
      face: "Eyes wide open (cute)"
      effect: "Small flash of inspiration line"
      posture: "Leaning forward"
    state: "Awakening"
    says: "Agents, this is insane..."
    micro_text: "(Wakes up here)"
    arrow_to:
      id: E
      shape: "gentle_curve"
      direction: "down_into_ring"

  # ===== Loop Start (Must rotate from here) =====
  - id: E
    zone: "loop"
    position: "loop_right_upper"
    actor: "Protagonist (Same person)"
    visual: "Holding head in hands (cute)"
    state: "Anxiety/Irritation Begins"
    says: "Irritated"
    thought_terms:
      - "Prompt Design"
      - "Tool Selection"
      - "Memory Design"
    thought_text: "It's not working as intended..."
    arrow_to: { id: F, shape: "curved_along_ring", direction: "clockwise" }

  - id: F
    zone: "loop"
    position: "loop_right_lower"
    actor: "Protagonist (Same person)"
    visual: "Surrounded by code and logs"
    state: "Irritation Settles In"
    says: "In a state of irritation"
    callout: "Loop! Runaway! Hallucination!"
    thought_terms:
      - "RAG"
      - "Function Calling"
      - "Tool-Use"
    arrow_to: { id: G, shape: "curved_along_ring", direction: "clockwise" }

  - id: G
    zone: "loop"
    position: "loop_bottom"
    actor: "Protagonist (Same person)"
    visual: "Covered in sticky notes and diagrams"
    says: "The body gradually develops tolerance, and usage increases."
    items:
      - "Agent Architecture Diagram"
      - "Role Assignment Memo"
      - "State Transition Flow"
    arrow_to: { id: H, shape: "curved_along_ring", direction: "clockwise" }

  - id: H
    zone: "loop"
    position: "loop_left_lower"
    actor: "Protagonist (Same person)"
    visual: "Works well, small fist pump"
    state: "Temporary Satisfaction"
    says: "Desire is temporarily satisfied"
    arrow_to: { id: I, shape: "curved_along_ring", direction: "clockwise" }

  - id: I
    zone: "loop"
    position: "loop_left_upper"
    actor: "Protagonist (Same person)"
    visual: "Thinking again"
    state: "Obsession/Thought Occupation"
    says: "Thinking about the agent
```

Brushed aluminum, white acrylic, or frosted glass backing

Panel 2 (Imperial Era): An Ottoman figure in the classical era, wearing an intricate Kaftan and a turban or janissary crest, with Istanbul's silhouette in the background.  Panel 3 (Independence Era): A 1923 Republic era officer or hero, wearing a traditional "Kalpak" hat and a vintage military wool uniform, representing the War of Independence.  Panel 4 (Modern Era): A confident modern-day character or futuristic pilot (Kızıl Elma style), representing the future of Türkiye.  Each panel shows the exact same character identity (same face structure and eyes) but adapted to the specific historical era, interacting subtly through poses and gazes looking at each other. Cinematic lighting, hyper-realistic, live action, 8k resolution, highly detailed textures. --ar 16:9
```

## 323. Kickstarter Campaign Generator Prompt

- **id**：2791
- **需要参考图**：是
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=2791

### 描述

A detailed system prompt instructing the model to act as a Senior Product Designer and Crowdfunding Campaign Manager. It analyzes an input image's material properties to automatically select and generate a concept for a modern smart wearable (Jacket, Backpack, or Sneaker) and then visualizes the concept in a precise 2x2 grid layout suitable for a Kickstarter campaign page, including a hero shot, detail shot, lifestyle shot, and campaign UI mockup.

### 示例图

![Kickstarter Campaign Generator Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1766385957583_svruzw_G8VpVYbXAAYij7H.jpg)

### 提示词

```
Act as a Senior Product Designer and Crowdfunding Campaign Manager.

1. ANALYZE INPUT
Look at the attached image. Determine the material properties (Soft vs. Hard), the primary colors, and the texture.

2. AUTO-SELECT SMART WEARABLE
Use this logic to choose the product category. The goal is Clean Modern Tech, not Cyberpunk.

IF the input is Soft or Fabric-like ({argument name="soft input examples" default="Ramen, Towel, Moss"})
GENERATE: The All-Climate Smart Jacket.
Features: Self-regulating temperature control, hydrophobic coating based on the input texture, and hidden magnetic pockets.

IF the input is Rigid, Boxy, or Container-like ({argument name="rigid input examples" default="Toaster, Book, Suitcase"})
GENERATE: The Ultimate Commuter Backpack.
Features: Integrated power bank with solar coating, anti-theft biometric zipper locks, and organized modular compartments.

IF the input is Mechanical or Footwear-shaped ({argument name="mechanical input examples" default="Stapler, Car, Tool"})
GENERATE: The Urban Mobility Smart Sneaker.
Features: Energy-returning sole technology, step-tracking sensors embedded in the heel, and auto-tensioning laces.

3. EXECUTE VISUALIZATION (2x2 GRID)
Generate a single image split into a precise 2x2 grid layout.

Top Left Panel (Hero Shot):
A clean, studio-lit 3/4 angle view of the product on a white or light grey background. It must look refined and manufacturable, using the texture of the input image as a premium material.

Top Right Panel (Detail Shot):
A zoomed-in macro shot focusing on a specific high-tech feature (e.g., a USB-C port, a magnetic buckle, or a waterproof seam) that shows off the input texture quality.

Bottom Left Panel (Lifestyle Shot):
A photorealistic shot of a fashionable model wearing the item in a clean, modern city setting (daytime). They look comfortable and stylish.

Bottom Right Panel (Campaign UI):
A mockup of a Kickstarter campaign page. It should show the product title, a green progress bar at 100% funded, and a bright green button that says Back This Project.

Overall Style: High-key lighting, soft shadows, 8k resolution, commercial product photography, minimalist design aesthetic.
```

# Instructions
Refer to the attached image and create an illustration of a "Character Relationship Chart" based on the following requirements.

# Production Style
- A warm, hand-drawn BD anime illustration style, similar to the image.
- White background.
- Characters should be drawn and placed inside circular icons.

# Character Settings
*List the order and characteristics corresponding to the uploaded images.
[Character A]
- Name: {argument name="Character A Name" default="Tsuno-chan"}
- Role: {argument name="Character A Role" default="Half-vampire"}

[Character B]
- Name: {argument name="Character B Name" default="Shiori-chan"}
- Role: {argument name="Character B Role" default="Loves new things"}

[Character C]
- Name: {argument name="Character C Name" default="Kumao-kun"}
- Role: {argument name="Character C Role" default="Always sleepy"}
[Character D]
- Name: {argument name="Character D Name" default="Donut"}
- Role: {argument name="Character D Role" default="Donut"}
(*Add more characters here if necessary)

# Specific Relationship List

from [A] to [B] : {argument name="Relationship A to B" default="Snack provider"}
from [A] to [C] : {argument name="Relationship A to C" default="Minion No. 1"}
from [A] to [D] : {argument name="Relationship A to D" default="Supreme item"}
from [B] to [A] : {argument name="Relationship B to A" default="Love!"}
from [B] to [D] : {argument name="Relationship B to D" default="Rival?"}
from [C] to [A] : {argument name="Relationship C to A" default="Don't want to get involved"}
from [C] to [B] : {argument name="Relationship C to B" default="Pitiful"}
from [C] to [D] : {argument name="Relationship C to D" default="It's a donut..."}
(*Add more relationships here if necessary)
# Relationship and Arrow Rules (Important)

Connect characters with arrows and strictly adhere to the following color-coding rules:
- Positive relationships (like, admiration, cooperation, etc.)

- Color: Red
- Atmosphere: Warm colors, may include hearts or sparkles.
- Negative relationships (dislike, hostility, nuisance, etc.)

- Color: Blue
- Atmosphere: Cold colors, may include jagged lines or cold sweat.
# Output Notes

- Arrange the layout in a balanced circular or hierarchical manner, even if the number of characters increases.
- Write the name and role below each character icon.
- Place the relationship description text near the arrow.
- If a relationship is not set, the arrow and text are unnecessary.
- Aspect ratio: 16:9
