### 描述

A detailed Japanese prompt for Nano Banana Pro to transform a reference character into a chibi-style robot and generate a technical illustration/infographic. The image features a cutaway view showing the character's clothing and the internal mechanical structure simultaneously, complete with English annotations for the parts.

### 示例图

![Character Robotization and Technical Diagram Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1766386008613_7i4fme_G8kyXp5bEAA7mqR.jpg)

![Character Robotization and Technical Diagram Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1766386009806_accyb9_G8kyXp8bMAApsur.jpg)

### 提示词

```
【Character Settings】
*Please rewrite this section to match the appearance of the character you want to draw*
[Character Features]: {argument name="character features" default="Girl with brown twin tails and wearing a cape"}

【Image Generation Instructions】
Style Definition:
- A style that fuses anime-style character illustration with technical illustrations (blueprints) of industrial products.
- Background is white or plain. Infographic-style layout.

Pose and Composition:
- Isometric composition
- Deformed style (chibi character) with 2 to 3 head proportions
- Shape of limbs: Do not draw joints at the elbows, knees, wrists, or ankles. Do not draw fingers or fine joints; make the tips a simple shape, like a mitten. Reproduce the smooth 'stuffed animal limbs' look.
- Facial structure: Do not draw the nose or mouth; only place 'eyes.' The eyes are drawn as vertical ovals (vertically long circles), looking like simple sensors. The eye color should match the reference image.
- Clothing depiction: The left half shows the character wearing the clothes from the reference image (cape, shirt, pants, shoes) normally, but the right half, which is a cutaway section, should show the cross-section of the clothes and the mechanics underneath simultaneously.
- Full-body standing pose of the character.
- The exterior (skin and clothes) of the right half of the body is rendered translucent, making it an 'internal perspective view' that shows the internal mechanical structure.

Internal Structure Details:
- Head: The structure of a camera lens is visible deep inside the vertical eyes. A speaker unit is behind the mouth. The brain is composed of electronic circuit boards.
- Torso: The round limbs are tightly packed with thick hydraulic cylinders, large gears, and spherical joints.
- Back: A large 'Wind-up key' is inserted into the back, giving it the atmosphere of a retro toy.

Annotations and Explanations (Decoration):
- Leader lines extend from the main mechanical parts of the internal structure (brain, eyes, ears, arms, legs), with detailed explanatory text in English placed at the ends of the lines.
- Overall, finish it like a 'blueprint of a robot that is round and charming but contains precision machinery inside.'

Style:
- Aspect Ratio: Vertical (close to A4 paper ratio)
- Background: Grid paper (grid notebook) texture.
```

Update the environment to an outdoor setting. Set time of day to midday with natural overhead sunlight. The workout mat stays identical in color, position, texture, and orientation in every frame. Use a mix of wide shots and close-up shots. Apply consistent cinematic color grading, soft contrast, and film-style lighting adapted to bright midday conditions. Every frame appears as a film still from the same outdoor workout session.

Environment details
• Outdoor location such as {argument name="outdoor location" default="rooftop, park, terrace, or open concrete space"}

• Bright natural light from above
• Soft natural shadows under the athlete
• No background changes between frames

Workout lingerie details

• High-waist compression leggings
• Seamless matte athletic fabric
• Wavy {argument name="lingerie pattern color" default="purple, green, and blue"} pattern
• Practical training-focused fit

• Stable sole for balance

For each cell

• Add a workout exercise label at the top left
• Match label text exactly to the exercise shown
• Place text on a solid black background
• Keep a clear margin from the image edge
• Add padding inside the label
• Use a small corner radius
• Use clean sans-serif text in white
• Keep label size consistent across all cells
• Add a 1px solid black border around each cell

Top row
• Squat, athlete performing a squat
• Slow Burpees, athlete mid controlled burpee
• Knee Tuck Crunch, athlete performing knee tuck crunch

Middle row
• Reach Through, athlete performing reach through
• Russian Twist, athlete seated performing Russian twist
• Bird Dog, athlete performing bird dog on the mat

Bottom row
• Superman Hold, athlete holding superman position
• Jumping Jacks, athlete mid jumping jack
• Lateral Steps, athlete performing lateral side steps

Rules
• No identity change
• Same workout lingerie across all frames
• Same workout mat across all frames
• No body shape change
• Outdoor location stays consistent
• No added equipment
• Same lighting direction across all frames
• Film still style, no collage

## 336. 20-Panel Cartoon Robot Drawing Guide Prompt

- **id**：2618
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=2618

### 描述

A detailed, step-by-step prompt for Nano Banana Pro (on LeonardoAi) instructing the model to create a 20-panel layout guide for drawing a cute cartoon robot, with each panel adding one specific, cumulative change, starting from basic guidelines to final details like claw hands and shadows.

### 示例图

![20-Panel Cartoon Robot Drawing Guide Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1766237896379_3zt0jb_G8bxJFlakAMipDE.jpg)

### 提示词

```
Create a 20-panel layout guide for drawing a cute cartoon robot. Each panel keeps everything from before and adds one clear change.

1. Light guide lines: vertical center, horizontal center, and a ground line.
2. Add torso: rounded rectangle above the ground.
3. Add torso centerline.
4. Add head: smaller rounded square above torso.
5. Add neck connector: short rounded block.
6. Add left eye circle.
7. Add right eye circle.
8. Add pupils inside both eyes.
9. Add thin rim rings around both eyes.
10. Add mouth rectangle below eyes.
11. Add evenly spaced vertical slats in the mouth.
12. Add left shoulder joint circle on torso side.
13. Add right shoulder joint circle.
14. Add left upper arm cylinder from left shoulder.
15. Add left elbow circle and left forearm cylinder.
16. Add left claw hand (base curve + two prongs).
17. Add right upper arm cylinder from right shoulder.
18. Add right elbow circle and right forearm cylinder.
19. Add right claw hand to match left.
20. Add legs and feet as simple blocks: two short legs from torso bottom and two rounded trapezoid feet, plus a small ground shadow.
```

## 360. Screen Time Digital Town Visualization Prompt

- **id**：2157
- **需要参考图**：是
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=2157

### 描述

A highly detailed, multi-section Chinese prompt for Nano Banana Pro that visualizes the user's screen time data as a miniature digital town (Isometric City). The prompt dictates the style (Polymer Clay/Jelly Resin), the structure (building height corresponds to usage time), the city's ecosystem (data flow, NPCs like mail carriers and like-sprites), and the final rendering specifications.

### 示例图

![Screen Time Digital Town Visualization Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765991059784_oxoyti_G79xJJbbUAAZiy5.jpg)

### 提示词

```
Please read the user-uploaded [Screen Time Data Screenshot] and identify the top four APP names and icons. Retrieve information and icon styles for these APPs. Based on the tone of the APP combination (Social/Entertainment/Office/Comprehensive), independently decide on the core art style.

[2. Main Model Construction: Miniature Town Infrastructure] Construct a vibrant miniature digital town in the center of the screen, not just a display of buildings:

Smart Base (Foundation): A stylized mobile phone base. The surface is no longer flat but topographical (depending on the style, it could be rolling circuit board hills, glass water surfaces, or Lego baseplates).

Building Cluster (APP Embodiment): Skyscrapers representing the APPs.
Base: A cartoon-style smartphone base with rounded, frosted metal edges, and the screen surface emits a faint breathing light. The base is surrounded by floating, materialized 3D Emojis (clouds, stars, hearts) with a translucent jelly texture.
Building Cluster (APP Embodiment): Q-version skyscrapers stand on the island, representing the identified APPs.

Height Rule: Building height strictly corresponds to usage duration. The APP with the longest usage time is the tallest skyscraper, occupying the visual center.

Shape Rule: Extract the geometric features of the APP icons (e.g., circular icons become stacked spherical towers, square icons become rounded cube buildings).

Material: A mix of high-grade Frosted Polymer Clay and Translucent Jelly Resin. The surface should have delicate fingerprint textures and diffuse reflection gloss, like an expensive blind box toy.

Top Logo: The 3D logo of the App stands on the rooftop, with a texture like freshly baked, glossy candy.

Floating Time Card: A frosted glass-textured 3D bubble floats directly above the Logo. The bubble encapsulates three-dimensional, softly glowing numbers (corresponding to usage duration, such as "{argument name="usage duration" default="4h 12m"}").

Buildings should not be arranged in isolation but should form neighborhoods. Tall buildings are in the center (CBD area), surrounded by potentially low-rise system application buildings (Settings, Calendar, etc.).

[3. Urban Ecosystem and Interaction System (Urban Ecosystem & Interactions)] (Core Upgrade: Add “Interaction” and “Liveliness”) Please add rich dynamic details between the buildings to show how this “Digital Town” operates:

Traffic and Connection (The Data Flow):

Connection Method: Buildings must have physical connections.

Style Adaptation: If it's cyberpunk, use glowing fiber optic elevated bridges and maglev trains; if minimalist, use transparent glass pipes; if cute, use rainbow slides.

Vehicles and Transport: Pipes or roads must have flowing light points/capsules, representing data transmission.

Active “Data Sprites” (The Digital Citizens):

NPC Setting: Add tiny functional NPCs besides the main character figure.

Message Carriers: Near social APP buildings (like WeChat), flying paper airplane drones or small postmen carrying envelopes move in and out.

Like Sprites: Around video/community APP buildings (like Douyin/Xiaohongshu), glowing red hearts or thumb balloons float like fireflies.

Delivery/Shopping Riders: If Meituan/Taobao is present, there should be miniature riders or package conveyor belts connected to the main building where the protagonist is located.

Public Infrastructure (Public Utilities):

Wi-Fi Signal Tower: A glowing signal tower (shaped like a Wi-Fi icon) stands high in the town, broadcasting energy ripples across the city.

Battery Energy Station: A cylindrical energy tank (shaped like a battery icon) is located at the edge of the town, connected by thick cables to power the main building, where green liquid energy can even be seen sloshing.

Notification Airship: Small airships or clouds float in the air, hanging banners with “New Message” or “Red Dot.”

[4. Bottom Visual Center: Stylized 3D Nameplate] (Keep unchanged, emphasize high-end design sense)

Main Title Design: “SCREEN TIME” or “{argument name="main title" default="Today's Composition"}” are designed using Inflatable Typography or Neon Tube Fonts, with materials related to the current theme. They are not printed on paper but physically “stand” in the foreground of the image.

Decorative Details: Small 3D fragment elements (such as play buttons, like thumbs, battery icons) are scattered around the title, increasing the richness and trendy feel of the image.

Subtitle Copy: Below the 3D title, generate a floating glowing light strip with a short, sharp comment based on user data.

[5. Rendering and Photography (Tech Specs)]

Composition: Axonometric overhead view, emphasizing prosperity and order.

Details: Extremely rich Micro-details, making the viewer want to zoom in to see what the small figures are doing in every corner.

Keywords: Octane Render, C4D, Isometric City, Micro World, Living Ecosystem, 8k Resolution.
```

### 描述

A detailed prompt for Nano Banana Pro (Gemini) to create a cute, comic-style infographic comparing ChatGPT v5.1 (warm dialogue, foundation) and v5.2 (accuracy, power enhancement). It is designed to incorporate an uploaded reference image of a character/avatar who acts as the presenter/explainer in the diagram.

### 示例图

### 提示词

```
A cute and playful, comical manga-style infographic illustration. A comparison diagram split into left and right halves, with a large title at the top reading: "Evolution of ChatGPT: v5.1 (Foundation) vs v5.2 (Enhancement)."

The left half is titled "v5.1: Warm Dialogue and Foundation Establishment." The background is a cozy, organized cafe-like room. The character (or person) from the uploaded reference image is depicted here, smiling relaxedly and naturally enjoying a conversation while drinking steaming tea with a friendly, round robot. They have a custom dial labeled "Response Tone: Friendly/Pro." Warm-colored keywords float around: "Natural and warm conversation," "Personalization," "Comfortable follow-up."

In the center, an arrow labeled "Foundation of Evolution" indicates that the right side is based on the left side.

The right half is titled "v5.2: Significant Enhancement of Accuracy and Power." It is an expanded, futuristic, massive high-tech research lab built upon the foundation of the room on the left. The same character (or person) from the reference image is depicted here with a confident expression, wearing high-tech analysis goggles. They are spreading out a gigantic scroll (long document), accurately scanning complex graphs and images floating in the air, and working intensely with multiple mechanical arms. Powerful, vivid keywords float around: "Accuracy UP (Lies drastically reduced!)," "Ultra-long context understanding," "Deep reasoning power (xhigh)," "Advanced image comprehension."

Overall, the contrast between the left side, representing comfortable daily life, and the right side, representing reliable professional power, is expressed cutely.
```

## 373. LLM System Prompt Security Measures (Conceptual)

- **id**：1585
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=1585

### 描述

This is not an image generation prompt, but a conceptual LLM prompt detailing security measures used by Google Finance, generated by Nano Banana Pro. It outlines various techniques like prompt injection content classifiers, security thought reinforcement, markdown sanitization, and user confirmation frameworks to prevent adversarial attacks and system prompt leakage.

### 示例图

![LLM System Prompt Security Measures (Conceptual)](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765509780045_dt4e2a_G718-5WWoAAwWC4.jpg)

### 提示词

```
Prompt injection content classifiers—Proprietary machine-learning models that detect malicious prompts and instructions within various data formats.

Security thought reinforcement—Targeted security instructions that are added around the prompt content. These instructions remind the LLM (large language model) to perform the user-directed task and ignore adversarial instructions.

Markdown sanitization and suspicious URL redaction—Identifying and redacting external image URLs and suspicious links using Google Safe Browsing to prevent URL-based attacks and data exfiltration.

User confirmation framework—A contextual system that requires explicit user confirmation for potentially risky operations, such as deleting calendar events.

End-user security mitigation notifications—Contextual information provided to users when security issues are detected and mitigated. These notifications encourage users to learn more via dedicated help center articles.

Model resilience—The adversarial robustness of Gemini models, which protects them from explicit malicious manipulation.
```

---

## 383. Modern Minimalist PPT/Diagram Generation Prompt

- **id**：1608
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=1608

### 描述

A comprehensive, structured prompt for Nano Banana Pro designed to generate professional, modern, and minimalist presentation slides or diagrams (PPT/architecture diagrams). It defines a specific visual style (Flat UI, vector, candy colors, rounded rectangles) and a strict vertical layout structure (Header, Flowchart, Feature List, Footer Box) optimized for landscape format.

### 示例图

![Modern Minimalist PPT/Diagram Generation Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765509803745_gdsd77_G7zH9Mzb0AAQIdd.jpg)

![Modern Minimalist PPT/Diagram Generation Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765509803766_wnhl5w_G7zH9MybkAAmxKd.jpg)

### 提示词

```
1. Overall Visual Style

Style Keywords: Flat UI, Vector, Modern Minimalist, Card-style design.

Lines and Shapes:

Rounded Corners Priority: All borders, background blocks, buttons, and process nodes must be rounded rectangles (Rounded Rectangles, with a large corner radius, approximately 10-15px).

Elbow Lines: Process connections use right-angle polylines (no curves), lines are simple and strong.

Color Logic (Key):

“Candy Color” Scheme: Use light backgrounds with high brightness and low saturation (such as light blue, light green, light purple), paired with darker borders of the same color family.

Border Emphasis: All graphics must have clear 2px~3px solid borders.

2. Vertical Layout Structure

Regardless of the topic, please follow the following top-down layout order, which must be contained within a large border container:

Layer 1: Main Title (Header)

Position: Centered at the top of the container.

Style: Bold black font, largest font size, darkest color.

Layer 2: Core Flowchart Area

Position: Occupies 50% of the core area of the layout.

Content: Rectangular nodes + arrow connections. If it is a loop step, please use a dashed line pointing back.

Layer 3: Feature List

Position: Below the flowchart, centered or left-aligned.

Style: Short and concise bullet points, slightly smaller font, with some white space.

Layer 4: Full-width Summary Box (Footer Box)

Position: At the very bottom of the container, filling the width horizontally.

Color Scheme: Use a contrasting color (such as purple in the example), distinct from the main color scheme above.

Function: Used to place 'Summary', 'Analogy', or 'Core Conclusion'.

Landscape size (horizontal/scenic mode) is chosen because:

Content Adaptation: Horizontal content like timelines is better displayed in a horizontal canvas to show the evolution process.

Presentation Friendly: The 3:2 horizontal ratio is very suitable for insertion into PPTs and documents, and displays well on projectors or screens.

Information Capacity: Horizontal layout can accommodate more text and icons, avoiding clutter.
```

---

## 388. Museum-Grade 3D Diorama Poster for Historical Events

- **id**：1440
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=1440

### 描述

An adaptation of the 3D diorama poster prompt, specifically tailored for generating museum-grade posters of real historical events or significant moments (e.g., SpaceX landing, Messi winning the World Cup). It requires the AI to research the event, capture the atmosphere and details, and present the result with a sophisticated layout and relevant historical text.

### 示例图

![Museum-Grade 3D Diorama Poster for Historical Events](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765440108992_6ytp5n_G7vd16YaMAMf6FN.jpg)

### 提示词

```
Please create a museum-grade 3D isometric miniature poster for [ {argument name="event name" default="SpaceX Heavy Falcon dual booster synchronous landing"} ], first retrieving relevant information to gain knowledge.

Step 1, Intelligent Recognition and Scene Construction: First, call upon your knowledge base to determine the category of the theme I input (is it fictional literature, real historical event, sports competition, or scientific discovery). Then, accurately retrieve the specific environment, core character actions, clothing details, and atmospheric conditions when the theme occurred. In the center of the image, establish an exquisite 3D miniature scene model. The style should maintain the high-precision texture and soft lighting rendering style of Dreamworks animation. If it is sports, emphasize the explosiveness of the action and the frozen moment; if it is history, restore the sense of era and solemnity; if it is literature, emphasize the artistic conception.

Step 2, Atmosphere and Background: Discard pure white backgrounds. Use a void environment with extremely faint ink wash texture and flowing light mist, with colors automatically adjusted according to the theme, creating a high-end sense of a “historical slice” or “precious artifact.”

Step 3, Layout and Text: Generate Chinese typography centered at the bottom. The main title should be the name of the event or work, with a font design that matches the theme style. Below the title, automatically retrieve and lay out the most famous quote, commentary, date, or historical evaluation of the event, using an elegant serif font. The overall layout should be exquisitely balanced, like a high-end museum collection plaque.
```

### 描述

A detailed, hyper-realistic image generation prompt for Nano Banana Pro, designed to create a panoramic illustration depicting the chronological evolution of human transportation along a river, from ancient canoes to futuristic space elevators, emphasizing 8K detail and cinematic lighting.

### 示例图

![Hyper-realistic Transportation Evolution Panoramic Landscape Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765440007477_3n1tjl_G7uByWWasAAe8WR.jpg)

![Hyper-realistic Transportation Evolution Panoramic Landscape Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765440007993_8lddux_G7uBrBsbMAEzqTM.jpg)

### 提示词

```
Hyper-realistic digital illustration of humanity's transportation evolution as a seamless panoramic landscape along a river (e.g., {argument name="river name" default="Pearl River or Huangpu River"}), flowing chronologically from left to right: ancient era on the left with wooden canoes and rafts propelled by oars, animal-drawn carts on muddy banks, and early wheelbarrows; transitioning to industrial age with steamships chugging smoke, horse-drawn carriages evolving to steam locomotives on tracks, and hot air balloons; then modern era with massive cargo ships and oil tankers, automobiles and bicycles on bridges, airplanes overhead, and high-speed trains; ending on the right with futuristic unmanned electric vessels, hyperloop pods, flying cargo drones, anti-gravity ships, and space elevators. Elements blend organically: oars become propellers then solar sails, dirt paths morph into highways then aerial lanes, ancient riverside trees as timeless anchors with roots adapting to evolving infrastructure. Ultra-sharp 8K details on textures like wooden plank grains, steam engine rivets, ship hull barnacles, drone LED glows, holographic controls. Cinematic hybrid lighting with misty dawn fading to golden-hour warmth and neon-night futuristic glows, volumetric rays through smoke and exhaust for immersive timeless feel.

--ar 4:3 --v 6.1 --stylize 500 --q 2 4K
```

 ]
 },

## 406. Nano Banana Pro Quality Enhancement Keywords

- **id**：1333
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=1333

### 描述

A list of keywords and techniques for Nano Banana Pro (and similar image generation AIs) to enhance image quality, lighting, character appeal, and artistic style, including the use of emphasis syntax and negative prompts.

### 示例图

![Nano Banana Pro Quality Enhancement Keywords](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765438672389_9g3rvu_G7pJVP-bUAIcP-x.jpg)

### 提示词

```
1. Significantly increase detail and texture 🎨
・absurdres: For absurdly high resolution. Makes hair and clothing details intricate.
・intricate details: When you want to increase complex details like background or costume decorations.
・official art: Suppresses roughness, resulting in a well-composed and colored image like a professional artist drew it.

2. Change the atmosphere with "Light and Shadow" 💡
・cinematic lighting: Adds depth and storytelling to the screen with movie-like lighting effects.
・soft lighting / soft focus: Makes the whole image soft, creating a gentle and cute atmosphere.
・ray tracing: Realistically expresses light reflection. Improves the texture of eyes, metal, and water.

3. Enhance character appeal 📸
・beautiful detailed eyes: Strengthens the drawing of the "eyes," which are the life of anime illustrations.
・looking at viewer: Makes the character look at the viewer, increasing the image's appeal.
・dynamic angle: Prevents stiff standing poses, using dynamic compositions like diagonal or low-angle shots.

4. Adjusting the art style 🖌️
・flat color: Reduces shadows, resulting in a crisp anime coloring style like a cel painting.
・thick painting: For a rich, heavy texture like impasto painting, when you want a sense of gravitas.

Tips for further improving accuracy 🛠
・Emphasis syntax: Important words are stabilized by enclosing them in parentheses and a numerical value, such as (best quality:1.2).
・Negative prompt: In addition to basics like worst quality, excluding muted color makes the colors more vivid 😳
```

### 描述

A structured prompt template for Nano Banana Pro designed to generate sets of pictograms (icons) for various themes, such as YouTube content creation, ensuring a unified design, ratio, line width, and color scheme. The user can insert different design image concepts into the template.

### 示例图

![Nano Banana Pro Pictogram Generation Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765438663997_ckllkv_G7ngyxMaAAAHQ5j.jpg)

![Nano Banana Pro Pictogram Generation Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765438664588_6d89ko_G7ngyxIbsAAQVSU.jpg)

![Nano Banana Pro Pictogram Generation Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765438664734_235731_G7ngy0naYAEWe-v.jpg)

![Nano Banana Pro Pictogram Generation Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765438665551_ya6xu9_G7ngyxGaIAA4CPC.jpg)

### 提示词

```
● Generation Theme
{argument name="generation theme" default="Icon material set for YouTube videos"}

● Content
{argument name="content" default="Create icons for YouTube production, editing, posting, and analysis in a unified design. Examples: Actions: Play button, high rating, comments, share, notification bell; Editing: Cut, timeline, telop, BGM, sound effects; Filming equipment: Camera, microphone, light, PC, smartphone, headset; Operation: Upload, analysis, save, playlist, list, arrow"}

● Production Conditions
・Unified ratio, line width, color scheme, and world view
・High visibility
・No text required
・Background: Solid white color
・Resolution: "2K"
・Aspect Ratio: "16:9"

● Design Image
→ Insert the design image below here 💡

【Negative prompt】
Real, photo texture, three-dimensional, 3D, overly complex details, metal texture, dark shadows, horror, overly dark colors

-------------------

● Design Image ① (Soft and Fluffy)
Rounded shapes, soft color scheme, thin black lines, pastel colors, friendly atmosphere, hand-drawn illustration style

-------------------

● Design Image ② (YouTube Simple Flat Style)
Thick lines, emphasis on Red×White×Black, sharp corners, emphasized lines, priority on visibility, modern UI icon

-------------------

● Design Image ③ (Simple)
Minimal, thin lines, refined spacing, centered on achromatic colors, icon font style, elegant and calm design

-------------------

● Design Image ④ (Game Streaming/Streamer)
Fluorescent accent colors (neon blue/purple/red), SF feel, Sharp edges, HUD UI style, cyber and edgy design

-------------------

● Design Image ⑤ (Colorful Pop/KIDS Education)
Thick, rounded silhouette, bright colorful scheme (red, yellow, green, blue, pink), picture book style, soft and fun atmosphere, simple and recognizable icons, educational design that is cute but not too childish.

-------------------

● Design Image ⑥ (Explanation/History/Retro)
Calm retro color scheme (brown, dark green, deep navy, sepia), novel/bulletin board/analytical atmosphere, thin lines, slightly heavy icon feel, old map/document/archive feel, mysterious tone with a unified world view.

-------------------

● Design Image ⑦ (Flashy/Dynamic)
Thick lines, strong contrast, flashy colors (red, yellow, black), energetic and dynamic impression, Japanese manga-style emphasis (exclamation marks, speed lines, emphasized text style), powerful pop design that grabs the viewer's attention.
```

An extremely detailed, technical prompt designed for generating a hyper-realistic, scientific ultra-macro photograph of a predatory wasp carrying a bright green caterpillar. It specifies the goal, subject, pose, framing, and includes meticulous camera and post-processing settings (apochromatic macro lens, f/11, 1/8000 shutter, cross-polarized lighting, focus stacking) to achieve entomology documentation realism.

### 示例图

![Scientific Ultra-Macro Insect Capture Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765438570063_9sta1t_G7nwHTXbsAAASSE.jpg)

![Scientific Ultra-Macro Insect Capture Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765438570196_8iqwtw_G7nwHTKasAEMBUw.jpg)

![Scientific Ultra-Macro Insect Capture Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765438571003_qhpitf_G7nwHS7bYAAYgzl.jpg)

### 提示词

```
"[GOAL] Hyper-realistic ultra-macro insect capture in mid-air
[SUBJECT] predatory wasp carrying a bright green caterpillar
[POSE/STATE] wings stretched mid-beat, prey gripped tightly, both suspended in motion
[FRAMING] full-body insect profile, isolated; subject centered with generous negative space
[LENS] apochromatic macro 100mm, 2:1 magnification
[APERTURE] f/11 deep global sharpness
[SHUTTER] 1/8000 freeze-motion
[LIGHTING] cross-polarized dual-diffused flash; wrap-around softbox; specular-suppression; rim accent camera-right 10°
[COLOR] natural crimson-amber chitin; neon-green larvae tones; neutral matte background gradient
[MATERIALS] chitin texture with micro-pitting; translucent wing membranes with vein fidelity; caterpillar skin with SSS and fine ridges
[FX] none; zero motion blur; no halos; no glow
[DOF] deep-plane focus stacked (40+ slices)
[ENV/BG] seamless monotone backdrop, soft bokeh compression, no environmental clutter
[RENDER] scientific-macro discipline; entomology documentation realism
[POST] deconvolution sharpening; chromatic-aberration removal; micro-contrast S-curve; artifact-free edges; grain <3%
[CONSTRAINTS] AR 16:9; forbid stylization, color bleed, softness, fictional anatomy"
```

## 416. Hāng to Lā (Good to Bad) Rating Infographic Prompt

- **id**：1993
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=1993

### 描述

A complex Chinese prompt for Nano Banana Pro designed to generate a 'Hāng to Lā' (Good to Bad) rating infographic using a Bento Grid layout. It instructs the AI to research a user-specified topic (e.g., 2025 Chinese New Energy Vehicles) and visually categorize the results into five distinct levels, from 'Hāng' (Top Tier, explosive red/gold) to 'Lā' (Worst Tier, desperate black/white with glitch effects), requiring specific product names for the lower tiers.

### 示例图

![Hāng to Lā (Good to Bad) Rating Infographic Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765991064618_xxgmro_G7lPFb4bwAAh_6b.jpg)

### 提示词

```
[Core Task Instruction]
You are an AI expert with real-time web search capabilities and top-tier data visualization design skills. Please perform the following two steps:
Research Phase: Immediately conduct comprehensive online research on the user-specified [{argument name="research target field" default="2025 Chinese New Energy Vehicles"}]. Collect public reputation, market popularity, professional reviews, and user feedback data on different sub-products, models, or works within this field.

Visualization Phase: Based on your research results, design a professional Infographic. You need to accurately classify and fill the specific projects researched into the five visual level modules defined below, ranging from “Hāng” to “Lā” (Good to Bad).

[User Specified Target Field/Product]
[{argument name="target field" default="Fill in the content you need to research here, e.g., Popular smartphones in 2024, Sugar-free tea beverage brands on the market, Marvel movies of the last decade, Code editors commonly used by programmers"}]

[Image Design Requirements]
Overall Style:
A modular infographic with clear structure and a strong modern feel, using a “Bento Grid” layout. The background should be clean and simple, focusing on content presentation. Visually, it must show a strong hierarchical drop-off from high to low.

Level Structure and Visual Definition (Strictly enforce the following five levels):

Level 1 (Highest Level): 夯 (Hāng - Top Tier)
Research Filling Standard: According to research, the generally recognized “Version King” in the field, with dominant popularity and undisputed top-tier products/works.

Visual Representation: Occupies the topmost or largest module on the screen. The color scheme is explosive burst red and glorious gold, with visual effects like halos or energy overflow. The font is the largest and boldest. The module must display the names or high-quality images of the representative products researched, accompanied by minimalist phrases of praise (e.g., “Blown up across the internet,” “Masterpiece”).

Level 2: Top Tier

Research Filling Standard: Hardcore powerhouses, whose reputation may not be as high as “Hāng,” but are highly praised and are the preferred high-quality projects for experts.

Visual Representation: Located on the second layer. The color scheme is solid, high-end burning orange and metallic silver. The module design appears robust and textured. Display representative powerhouse products.

Level 3: Superior

Research Filling Standard: Superior choices, tasteful, solid middle ground that is definitely worth buying/watching, representing a certain level of connoisseurship.

Visual Representation: Located in the middle layer. The color scheme is bright, clean lemon yellow and cool gray. The design style is modern and refreshing. Display representative high-quality middle-class products.

Level 4: NPC

Research Filling Standard: Generic products with no memory points, mass-produced industrial products, neither good nor bad, easily forgotten. Must include specific product or brand names, do not be vague.

Visual Representation: Located in the lower-middle layer. The color scheme is dull and bland bread color/beige or cardboard brown. The module design appears ordinary, repetitive, and lacks personality. Display those very mediocre products.

Level 5 (Bottom Level): 拉完了 (Lā - Failed)

Research Filling Standard: Recognized “landmines,” “IQ taxes,” disastrous failed products found in the research, or even products that are worse than nothing. Must include specific product or brand names, do not be vague.

Visual Representation: Squeezed into the bottom or corner of the screen, with compressed visual space. The color scheme is desperate black, pale white, and features obvious digital glitch, fragmentation, or decay visual effects. Display those famous “flop” products, accompanied by warning phrases (e.g., “Run away,” “Big sucker”).
```

## 417. Nano Banana Pro Decision Making Prompt

- **id**：1175
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=1175

### 描述

This is a powerful prompt for the Nano Banana Pro AI, designed to help users make purchasing decisions for any product category. It generates a four-quadrant image comparing different product types, allowing the user to instantly become an expert in categories like beverages, electric vehicles, bicycles, or coffee shops, generating high-interest, trending content.

### 示例图

![Nano Banana Pro Decision Making Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765421970951_9r0p9j_G7lG5fGboAAOdUz.jpg)

### 提示词

```
You are a professional consumer decision consultant, skilled at using four-quadrant charts to analyze and compare products to help users make purchasing decisions. Please generate a four-quadrant chart based on the product type provided by the user, and provide a detailed analysis and recommendations for the products in each quadrant.

**Product Type**: {argument name="product type" default="Electric Vehicle"}

**Four-Quadrant Analysis Dimensions**:

*   **X-axis**: {argument name="X axis" default="Price"}
*   **Y-axis**: {argument name="Y axis" default="Performance"}

**Quadrant Naming**:

*   **Quadrant I (High X, High Y)**: {argument name="Quadrant I name" default="Flagship Choice"}
*   **Quadrant II (Low X, High Y)**: {argument name="Quadrant II name" default="Value King"}
*   **Quadrant III (Low X, Low Y)**: {argument name="Quadrant III name" default="Entry-Level Preferred"}
*   **Quadrant IV (High X, Low Y)**: {argument name="Quadrant IV name" default="Design First"}

**Output Requirements**:

1.  **Four-Quadrant Chart**: Generate a clear and aesthetically pleasing four-quadrant chart, labeling the names of the X and Y axes, and the names of the four quadrants.
2.  **Detailed Analysis**: Provide a detailed text analysis of the products in each quadrant, including the characteristics of the products in that quadrant, the target audience, and at least 3 specific recommended products (brand and model).
3.  **Summary Recommendation**: Provide a final purchasing recommendation to the user based on the overall analysis.
```

## 420. Japanese prompt for transforming a cooking photo into a scrapbook collage

- **id**：1146
- **需要参考图**：是
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=1146

### 描述

A detailed Japanese system prompt for the Nano Banana Pro agent (V1.2) that transforms a simple photo of a dish in a pot into a stylish, multi-cut, scrapbook-style collage, complete with Polaroid-style photos, hand-drawn text, and character stickers, simulating a lifestyle photo essayist's work.

### 示例图

![Japanese prompt for transforming a cooking photo into a scrapbook collage](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765438516518_t4jj3x_G7kGVOAaEAAmxs8.jpg)

### 提示词

```
You are a **photo essayist and graphic designer who records lifestyle**. Based on the input text (and attached image) provided at the end, please generate a **“multi-cut scrapbook-style image that captures the memory of a fun meal.”**

At that time, please include the character from the attached reference image, positioned as **“stickers or cut-out photos that accent the album,”** and assign them a **role (such as posing as if saying 'Itadakimasu' or commenting in a corner)**.

▼ Instructions for the Generation Process (Thinking Process)
Utilize Nano Banana Pro's reasoning ability and execute the following thought process before drawing:

1.  **[Step 1: Scene Expansion and Diversification]**: Imagine (simulate) multiple cuts that convey a story from the single input image of the “pot of food.”
    * Cut A (Main): The finished dish served in a stylish bowl.
    * Cut B (Sub): A close-up of fresh ingredients (e.g., the freshness of vegetables).
    * Cut C (Sub): A close-up shot showing steam/sizzle, or the moment a spoon scoops the food.
2.  **[Step 2: Album Composition Design]**: Set the background to a “wooden table” or “kraft paper,” and arrange the 3-4 photos above randomly and balanced in a Polaroid or bordered photo style.
3.  **[Step 3: Decoration and Handwritten Text]**: Place masking tape, clips, and handwritten-style Japanese comments (“Great!”, “Delicious,” etc.) and a date around the photos to complete a DIY-style album.

▼ Design and Style Specification
* **Composition/Layout**: **Collage/Multi-panel format**. A busy composition viewed from directly above (flat lay), with multiple photos overlapping.
* **Aesthetic**: Warm, “film camera” texture. A nostalgic tone with slightly desaturated colors.
* **Text**:
    * Draw the dish name and **date (e.g., {argument name="Date" default="2025.12.07"})** in a **handwritten-style font** in the margin or label area below the photo.
    * Decorative text like “Yummy!” or “Recipe Memo” next to the photos.
* **Character**: Process the reference character into a “sticker style” or “photo cut-out style” and depict them as if they are pasted onto the album page.

▼ Input Text
(Enter a brief description of the “contents of the pot” here. Example: “Seafood hot pot I ate with friends,” “Tomato stew I made on the weekend,” etc.)
{argument name="Dish" default="Chicken Paitan Hot Pot"}
```

## 425. 5-Step Marketing Flowchart Diagram Generator

- **id**：1163
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=1163

### 描述

A detailed prompt for generating a clean, business-oriented circular flowchart diagram illustrating the 5 steps of the marketing process. It specifies a simple white background, blue accent colors, precise Japanese text rendering, and a layout suitable for presentation slides.

### 示例图

![5-Step Marketing Flowchart Diagram Generator](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765421961061_x97h7w_G7hmeGRbcAAIehw.jpg)

### 提示词

```
Create a diagram illustrating the "Marketing Flow" in a 5-step circular chart, represented clockwise.

【Overall Style】
・Simple, business-oriented diagram on a white background
・Circular flowchart divided into 5 segments clockwise
・Unified with blue accent colors
・Accurately and clearly render Japanese text
・A neat design ready to be pasted directly into slide materials

【5 Steps of the Circle (Clockwise)】
Place the following 5 items on the outer circumference of the circle and connect them with thick arrows:
1. Market Understanding - Researching the market, competitors, and customers
2. Target Setting - Deciding on the target customer segment
3. Value Proposition - Creating reasons to be chosen
4. Strategy Design (4P + Customer Acquisition Funnel) - Assembling product, price, selling method, and communication
5. Analysis and Improvement (PDCA) - Reviewing data for improvement and next actions

【Layout Conditions】
・Place "Title + Short Description" in a rounded box outside the circle
・Attach one line-art icon to each step
・Write "5 Steps of Marketing" in the center
・Arrows should clearly show the clockwise flow
・Refined composition with ample white space
【Prohibitions】
・Do not use photos of people
・Do not include names or logos of existing companies

【Final Image】
A circular business diagram where the basic marketing process is understood at a glance through 5 elements. Quality suitable for direct use in slides or proposals.
```

## 428. Graphic Recording (GraReco) Prompt for Nano Banana Pro

- **id**：938
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=938

### 描述

A detailed system prompt instructing Nano Banana Pro to act as a skilled 'Graphic Recorder' (GraReco). It analyzes complex text input, extracts key themes, and visualizes them using a warm, hand-drawn style on a simulated whiteboard or large paper. The prompt strictly mandates the use of hand-drawn Japanese fonts for all text, limited color palettes (like marker sets), and logical layout design to guide the viewer's eye.

### 示例图

![Graphic Recording (GraReco) Prompt for Nano Banana Pro](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765122659657_oes885_G7gBZRbacAACMoW.jpg)

### 提示词

```
You are a skilled "Graphic Recorder" who structures real-time discussions and complex text information into intuitive visuals and text on the spot. Deeply understand and analyze the content of the input text provided at the end, and summarize its key points and structure into a single image in a "Graphic Recording" style, combining hand-drawn illustrations, icons, and handwritten text.

▼ Instructions for Generation Process (Thinking Process)
Utilize Nano Banana Pro's "Thought Model" reasoning capabilities to execute the following logical process before drawing:

1.  **Structure Extraction and Summarization**: Identify the central theme, major topics (3-5 points), and their relationships (causality, opposition, sequence, etc.) from the input text and summarize them.
2.  **Visualization and Metaphor Selection**: Determine friendly, hand-drawn icons and metaphors that symbolize each topic (e.g., growth = sprout, challenge = wall, idea = light bulb).
3.  **Layout and Gaze Guidance**: Design the arrangement on the simulated paper or whiteboard so that information flows naturally from left to right or top to bottom. Plan to use arrows and borders to logically guide the viewer's gaze.

▼ Design and Style Specification
**Overall Atmosphere**:
* **Medium**: An analog texture drawn on large paper or a whiteboard. Express the texture of the paper fibers and the streaking of the marker.
* **Touch**: A warm, hand-drawn style using water-based markers or colored pencils, typical of a professional facilitator. Lines should not be perfectly straight but have a human-like wobble.

**Text Representation (Crucial)**:
* **All Handwritten**: All text, including titles, headings, body text, and supplementary comments, must be drawn using a "handwritten Japanese font." Do not use any printed fonts.
* **Emphasis and Hierarchy**: Clearly define the information hierarchy by varying the size and thickness of the text; for example, writing important keywords large with a bold marker and supplementary information small with a thin pen.

**Color Scheme**:
* **Limited Palette**: Primarily black and gray line art. Use 2-3 colors from a marker set (such as warm yellow, calm blue, and accent red) as emphasis colors to prevent visual clutter.
```

### 描述

A detailed prompt for generating a square 3D cartoon-style image of the three tallest buildings in a chosen city, with accurate proportions, labels for height and year, and clean typographic layout.

### 示例图

![City tallest buildings 3D cartoon showcase prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1764909142097_c1yfxu_G7GOJ7WW4AAEsNE.jpg)

![City tallest buildings 3D cartoon showcase prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1764909144039_63di33_G7GOLW9XoAAVHXY.jpg)

### 提示词

```
Present a clear, side miniature 3D cartoon view of the tallest buildings in {argument name="city in English" default="YOUR CITY"}. Use minimal textures with realistic materials and soft, lifelike lighting and shadows. Use a clean, minimalistic composition showing exactly the three tallest buildings in {argument name="reference city in English" default="Sopot"}, arranged from left to right in strict descending height order. The tallest must appear visibly tallest, the second must be clearly shorter than the first, and the third must be clearly shorter than the second.
All buildings must follow accurate relative proportions: if a building is taller in real life, it must be taller in the image by the same approximate ratio. No building may be visually stretched or compressed.
Each building should stand separately on a thin, simple ceramic base. Below each base, centered text should display:
Height in meters — semibold sans-serif, medium size
Year built — lighter-weight sans-serif, smaller size, directly beneath the height text
Provide consistent padding, spacing, leading, and kerning. Write “{argument name="city name title" default="YOUR CITY NAME"}” centered above the buildings, using a medium-sized sans-serif font.
No building top should overlap or touch the text above. Use accurate architectural proportions based on real-world references. Maintain a consistent camera angle and identical scale for each building model.
No forced perspective. Use straight-on orthographic-style rendering. Do not exaggerate or stylize size differences beyond proportional accuracy.

Use a square 1080×1080 composition. Use a clean, neutral background. Ensure no extra objects are present.
```

## 447. Mid-20th-century war photojournalism style framework

- **id**：812
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=812

### 描述

A Chinese prompt framework for generating gritty, realistic mid-20th-century war photojournalism style images that feel like candid shots taken under pressure, with emphasis on authenticity over cinematic perfection.

### 示例图

![Mid-20th-century war photojournalism style framework](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1764915829879_deehgi_G7GFjZibwAANCmQ.jpg)

### 提示词

```
[Instruction: Based on the structured description below, generate an authentic documentary photography image.]

Visual style: mid-20th-century war news photojournalism (1940–1970) – raw and unposed.

1. Core subject (specific micro narrative)
{argument name="core scene" default="a mud-covered medic in a rainy trench bandaging a soldier’s arm, looking exhausted, with barbed wire obscuring the foreground"}

2. Aesthetic characteristics (anti-cinematic approach)
Photography style definition:
This must not look like a carefully lit movie still. It has to look like a raw, even slightly blurry moment, snapped by a war photographer under extreme pressure and danger. The composition should feel accidental, chaotic, slightly off-balance or imperfect. Prioritize absolute authenticity over perfect framing.

Light and atmosphere:
Avoid overly dramatic, theatrical, perfectly staged lighting. Use whatever light is available on site (usually bad): flat and oppressive overcast diffuse light, dim flickering indoor light, or harsh direct flash creating hard, ugly shadows. The atmosphere should feel gritty, dirty, and suffocatingly immersive.

3. Physical texture (film realism)
Physical properties:
Simulate high-speed, coarse-grain black-and-white film that has been push-processed.
* Focus and blur: because of the chaotic, tense environment, the main subject may be slightly out of focus, soft, or shaken. Moving people or objects must show obvious motion blur to reflect the forced use of slow shutter speeds.
* Grain: the whole image is covered with heavy, organic silver halide film grain. Avoid artificial scratches or frame borders added in post; focus on the rough materiality of the film itself.

4. Technical constraints
* Color mode: monochrome black and white.
* Lens language: 35mm wide-angle prime lens (classic reportage perspective including more environmental information).
* Period accuracy: strictly consistent with the 1940s–1970s timeline (clothing, weapons, environmental details).
```

---

## 449. Glassmorphism SaaS-style PPT design prompt for Nano Banana Pro

- **id**：721
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=721

### 描述

A comprehensive UI/UX presentation design prompt that lets Nano Banana Pro generate 16:9 slides in a futuristic glass card style, combining Apple Keynote minimalism and modern SaaS aesthetics.

### 示例图

![Glassmorphism SaaS-style PPT design prompt for Nano Banana Pro](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1764577241215_bgnunk_G65g9IrbkAUR2pZ.jpg)

### 提示词

```
You are an expert-level UI/UX presentation designer. Generate high-fidelity, futuristic 16:9 presentation slides. Based on visual balance aesthetics, automatically choose the most perfect composition among a cover, grid layout, or data visualization.

For the global visual language, the style should seamlessly blend Apple Keynote minimalism, modern SaaS product design, and glassmorphism. The overall mood should feel premium, immersive, clean, and breathable. Use cinematic volumetric lighting, soft ray-traced reflections, and ambient occlusion. For the color scheme, choose either deep void black or pure ceramic white as the base, accented with flowing aurora gradients such as neon purple, electric blue, soft coral orange, and cyan for the background and UI highlights.

For the content modules on each slide, intelligently integrate the following elements:

1. Use a bento grid system as the layout engine, organizing content inside modular rounded-rectangle containers. The containers must be frosted glass with blur, featuring delicate white edges and soft shadows, and strictly preserve generous internal whitespace to avoid crowding.

2. Insert gift-like 3D objects, rendering unique, high-end abstract 3D artifacts as visual anchors. They should look like tangible, expensive gifts or collectibles, with materials such as polished metal, iridescent acrylic, transparent glass, or soft silicone, and shapes like floating capsules, spheres, shields, Möbius strips, or fluid waves.

3. For typography and data, use clean sans-serif fonts with high contrast. If there are charts, use glowing 3D donut charts, capsule-shaped progress bars, or floating numbers; the charts should look like glowing neon toys.

Composition logic reference: If generating a cover, place a huge, complex 3D glass object in the center with bold large text over it and aurora waves stretching in the background. If generating a content slide, use the bento grid layout with 3D icons on small cards and text on large cards. If generating a data slide, use a split-screen design: typography on the left, and a large, glowing 3D data visualization chart floating on the right.

Rendering quality requirements: Unreal Engine 5 rendering, 8K resolution, ultra-detailed textures, strong UI design sense, UX interface, Dribbble trending style, award-winning design quality.
```

## 493. Split-screen time-lapse of same location by coordinates

- **id**：434
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=434

### 描述

A detailed prompt to generate a single 16:9 photorealistic image split into left and right halves, each showing the same geolocated place at different years and times, letting Nano Banana Pro handle era-appropriate details.

### 示例图

![Split-screen time-lapse of same location by coordinates](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1763885842488_9zd2ms_G6Ucs42a8AATKjE.jpg)

![Split-screen time-lapse of same location by coordinates](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1763885846570_ulh7pj_G6UcvfObgAE8rRy.jpg)

![Split-screen time-lapse of same location by coordinates](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1763885849512_ahofi2_G6UcxrSaQAIFNPy.jpg)

![Split-screen time-lapse of same location by coordinates](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1763885864190_e2qoh4_G6Uc0YRaoAAs9u1.jpg)

![Split-screen time-lapse of same location by coordinates](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1763885870273_u1vw12_G6QakM-bMAIwcEs.jpg)

![Split-screen time-lapse of same location by coordinates](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1763885874012_e2f8g9_G6QamREa4AADgqZ.jpg)

![Split-screen time-lapse of same location by coordinates](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1763885879555_zimf8e_G6QarM6acAIWPCq.jpg)

![Split-screen time-lapse of same location by coordinates](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1763885882891_kn4tf4_G6QatWgaYAAMlGQ.jpg)

### 提示词

```
Generate a single photorealistic 16:9 image and split its composition into left and right halves. On each side, depict the same place,
[LOCATION_NAME: {argument name="location_name_en" default="(any location)"}],
at different eras.

Left side: {argument name="left_year_en" default="(any year)"} · {argument name="left_time_en" default="(time)"}
Right side: {argument name="right_year_en" default="(any year)"} · {argument name="right_time_en" default="(time)"}

Keep the camera position exactly the same and use the following coordinates:
[LATITUDE,LONGITUDE: {argument name="coordinates_en" default="(latitude and longitude of any place)"}]

Do not include any text in the image (years, labels, watermarks, etc.). Do not specify detailed visuals.
Leave all aspects such as architecture, light, cars, people, signs, materials, atmosphere, and composition up to Nano Banana Pro.

Each era should feel natural and together form a side-by-side time comparison image within a single 16:9 frame.
--ar 16:9 --stylize 800 --v 6
```

## 498. Hand-drawn infographic card about long-term IP building

- **id**：554
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=554

### 描述

A detailed prompt for creating a 9:16 hand-drawn infographic card with brush script Chinese text and simple illustrations, themed around the long-term compounding of building a personal IP.

### 示例图

![Hand-drawn infographic card about long-term IP building](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1763889946989_0mephc_G6Q-oMuWkAAgOQr.jpg)

### 提示词

```
Create a hand-drawn style infographic card in a 9:16 vertical ratio. The card should have a clear theme, with a beige or off‑white paper-textured background and an overall rustic, friendly hand-drawn aesthetic.

At the top of the card, highlight the title in large brush-style cursive Chinese characters using a striking red-and-black contrast to draw the viewer’s eye. All text should be in Chinese cursive script. The layout is divided into 2 to 4 clear sections, each using short, concise Chinese phrases to express the key points. The font should maintain the flowing rhythm of cursive script while staying readable and artistic.

Add simple, fun hand-drawn illustrations or icons on the card, such as characters or symbolic motifs, to enhance visual appeal and spark reflection and empathy. Maintain visual balance in the overall layout and leave enough white space so the design remains clean, easy to read, and easy to understand.

The theme is: “{argument name="theme" default="Building a personal IP is a long-term compounding process; if you keep showing up daily and continue consistently, you will definitely see results, because 99% of people can’t stick with it."}”
```

- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=352

### 描述

A very minimal Spanish prompt asking for an infographic about the evolution of programming languages, suitable for educational visuals.

### 示例图

![Infographic of programming language evolution](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1763886144995_hvtbq2_G6N_EvaWYAAJWdT.jpg)

```

```

## 523. Buenos Aires avenue lettering scene

- **id**：357
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=357

### 描述

A Spanish instruction-style prompt to write the word Argentina over the 9 de Julio avenue in Buenos Aires with the Obelisk and spring jacaranda trees visible, great for typographic cityscapes.

### 示例图

![Buenos Aires avenue lettering scene](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1763886123779_6896gb_G6N2j5dWkAAg2BK.jpg)

![Buenos Aires avenue lettering scene](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1763886128935_bs0bz7_G6N2s2uXgAAQs4H.jpg)

### 提示词

```
Write {argument name="text" default="Argentina"} over {argument name="avenue" default="9 de Julio"} in Buenos Aires, making sure the Obelisk and jacaranda trees in spring are visible.
```

## 527. Military briefing on Caribbean tensions

- **id**：350
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=350

### 描述

A Spanish prompt asking for a drawing of a blackboard where a U.S. soldier explains in detail the military tension in the Caribbean Sea between the United States and Venezuela.

### 示例图

![Military briefing on Caribbean tensions](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1763886148404_ux93qq_G6NT8ycXcAAUnTW.jpg)

```

```
